# F10 — The player catches and explains every state

Status: in-progress (merged F10 branch at 8273020: S1–S8, S4b–S4k, S6/S7/S6m/S6n/S6r/S6s; S4l (JS half of the live native audit S9b turn 2) done 2026-10-06 in `../streamarr-f10`, not committed; native half in `../streamarr-f10n`; live checks S9)
Status: in-progress (S1–S5, S4b–S4d, S8, S9a fixes, B13b committed up to 4593df6; S4f+S4g committed as 296aa6b; S4h (the player's words) and S4i (S9a2 web re-audit fixes) done 2026-10-05 in `../streamarr-f10`, not committed; S6/S7 native probes in the parallel worktree `../streamarr-f10n`; live checks S9)

## Intent
Implement the user requirement of 2026-10-05 00:50 (PLAN § 5 "I"): the viewer never sees a generic error, a black
screen or a frozen picture. Every player state is either compensated automatically (wait, reload, new start at the
position, lower quality, step-down, other version) or explained with one specific de/en hint and an action. One
health watchdog per engine (frames, clock, audio), one error taxonomy with a compensation ladder and budgets, jest
tests per matrix row with fake engines, then live runs with Dev World fault injection (B12) on every engine.

F10a (research + design, no devices) is done; F10 S1–S9 are the build slices.

## Server follow-ups found (not part of F10/B12)
- Seek back > 15 min inside one long transcode run waits on a run that never rewrites the retained-away segment →
  90 s hang → `504` (inferred from `TranscodeSessionManager.cs:358-366`, `:614-633`; not reproduced).
- Audio fallback flag for `/switch` (convert the selected track to AAC) for the ladder step **A**.
- Optional `playbackAlive` in the progress answer (a dead `playbackId` answers 200 today).
- `Retry-After` on `503 segment_evicted`; a per-wait cap (~25 s) so client and server timeouts agree.

## Slice S1 — taxonomy + texts (code only, no devices)

**What.** One category for every failure and a specific text for every code, including codes the client has never
seen.

| Part | Where |
|---|---|
| `ERROR_CATEGORIES` T1–T11, `CODE_CATEGORY` (total over every known code, type-checked), `categoryOfStatus`, `categoryOf(code, status?)` (known code → table; unknown → HTTP status → name rule → T11) | `client/src/api/error-categories.ts` (new) |
| `classify(source)` for API answers, engine errors (hls.js `<type>:<details>` + HTTP status, `<video>` MediaError 1–4 / Chrome pipeline texts, ExoPlayer messages incl. `Response code: NNN`, `ERROR_CODE_IO_*`, decoder/AudioSink/parser/reclaim/DRM, AVPlayer `NSURLError`/CoreMedia `-12927`/`-12642`…, VLC), watchdog verdicts, system causes, exceptions | `client/src/player/recovery/classify.ts` (new) |
| New codes with de/en texts: media delivery (`unknown_stream`, `stream_capacity`, `unknown_transcode`, `unknown_segment`, `end_of_stream`, `session_closed`, `segment_evicted`, `init_unavailable`, `segment_unavailable`, `too_many_sessions`, `ffmpeg_unavailable`, `transcoding_disabled`, `invalid_transcode_request`, `remux_not_possible`, `no_video_stream`, `unknown_duration`, `viewer_not_found`), engine codes (`decode_error`, `encrypted_media`, `player_load_failed`, `video_stalled`, `engine_error`, `unexpected_format`, `decoder_reclaimed`), `device_caps_unavailable` | `client/src/api/error-codes.ts`, `en.json`/`de.json` `errors.codes` (appended at the end of the section) |
| Category texts `errors.categories.T1…T11` (title + message, de/en) | `en.json`/`de.json` `errors.categories` (new, end of `errors`) |
| Unknown-code fallback: `describeError` never returns `errors.codes.unknown` for an unknown code; it returns the category text chosen by HTTP status (else name, else T11); the card's existing "Error code {code}" line shows the raw code. `ErrorLike`/`ErrorState`/`FailedState` carry `status`; the controller's catch paths pass `appError.status` | `api/error-text.ts`, `components/states/error-state.tsx`, `player/controller.ts`, `screens/player/play-screen.tsx` |
| `switchFailed` notice carries the failed request's status (`params.status`) → `noticeError()` → category text | `player/controller.ts` (`lastErrorStatus`), `player/overlay-labels.ts`, `play-screen.tsx` |
| Device caps failure → own code `device_caps_unavailable` instead of `unknown` (+ `console.warn` in dev) | `play-screen.tsx` |
| Hint catalogue `H.*` (22 hints, `{cause}` texts for system pauses and slow starts, 11 actions; de/en; ICU number format for Mbit/s and %) and `HINT_ACTIONS`, `hintText()` | `client/src/player/recovery/hints.ts` (new), `player.en.json`/`player.de.json` `hints` (new section at the end) |
| Drift test: every code of `docs/api.md` §5 + §11 (status chains `` `404 a` / `b` `` and the `422` planning list) is known, translated and has a category; §12/§13 were already covered | `client/src/api/__tests__/errors.test.ts` |

**Per finding.**

| Row | Root cause (code) | Fix | Test (fails before the fix) | State |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| B12 | `describeError` mapped every code outside `error-codes.ts` to `unknown` (`error-text.ts:16` before) → "Something went wrong" | category fallback by HTTP status / name; `status` threaded from `AppError` to the card | `matrix/B.test.ts` "B12 …" (server answers `500 brand_new_code` → card text = T6, not generic; 12 status/name cases) and `errors.test.ts` "unknown codes get their category text" — the old test asserted the generic text, so both fail on the old code | fixed in code; live check pending (S2) |
| E15 | `switchFailed` reason = `describeError({code})` → generic for unknown codes | same fallback + the status in the notice params | `matrix/E.test.ts` "E15 …" (switch answers `503 brand_new_code` → reason = T4 message) — fails before (generic, and no `status` param) | fixed in code; live check pending (S2) |
| E16 (part) | `loadDeviceCaps` rejection → `toAppError(error).code` = `unknown` → generic card | own code `device_caps_unavailable` with de/en text | `matrix/E.test.ts` "E16 …" (source uses the code; texts not generic) | text fixed; static-profile fallback → S3 (pending row) |
| A04 (part) | `refresh_session_revoked` / `refresh_token_unknown` unknown to the client → generic title on the profiles screen | category fallback: `refresh_*` → T3 "Signed out" until F8's own texts land | `matrix/A.test.ts` "A04 …" | no longer generic; F8 adds the code texts; player card `H.signedOut` → S4 |
| A06 (part) | as A04 with `reason: account_disabled` | as A04 | `matrix/A.test.ts` "A06 …" | as A04 |

Decisions:
- `categoryOf` lives in `api/` (used by `describeError` on every screen); `classify.ts` builds on it. Unknown code
  without status: name rules (`refresh_*`/`revoked` → T3, `capacity|busy|too_many` → T4, `timeout` → T5,
  `not_allowed|disabled|restricted|forbidden` → T9, `*_not_found|unknown_*|*_closed|*_expired` → T2,
  `unavailable|*_failed|server` → T6, `codec|decod|unsupported|format` → T7), else T11.
- HTTP 503 of the **API** → T4 (busy); 503 of a **media request** → T6 (`segment_unavailable`, matrix C14). 504 → T5.
- Watchdog `slideshow` → T5 (b.1 lists it under T5; § a says T7 — T5 matches its ladder "lower quality"); `starting`
  (no first picture) → T7 per § c "no frames at all → T7".
- An engine error nothing recognises stays T7 `engine_error` (today's step-down behaviour; the ladder is S4).
- `errors.codes.unknown` stays for genuine client exceptions (`toAppError` of a non-AppError); every *server* code the
  client does not know now gets a category text.
- The two B11 refresh codes are not added here (F8 owns them); after the merge they get their own texts and
  `CODE_CATEGORY` entries (T3) — `CODE_CATEGORY` is a `Record<KnownErrorCode, …>`, so tsc fails until they are mapped.
- No player behaviour changed (no ladder, no status UI): only texts, classification and the `status` passed along.

## Live checks for S2 (later message; one device at a time, Dev World with B12 faults)

1. **B12 / E15 (web, hls.js, Chrome `--mute-audio`)**: arm `api_status` `{ scope: {next: viewer}, target: "api:start",
   params: {status: 500, code: "brand_new_code"} }`, start any title → card title "The server had a problem", line
   "Error code brand_new_code" (de: "Problem auf dem Server"). Then play normally, arm `api_status` on `api:switch`
   `{status: 503, code: "brand_new_code"}`, pick another quality → notice "Couldn't switch. The server is handling too
   much right now. …" (T4), playback continues. Repeat once on Android TV (Exo) for the card.
2. **A04 / A06 (profiles screen)**: `session_revoke {viewer, reason: account_disabled}` → next refresh refused → the
   account row shows "Signed out" (T3 title, until F8's own text lands), never "Something went wrong".
3. **E16**: not reproducible without breaking media-caps; covered by the unit test (caps rejection is native).
4. Screenshot evidence under `docs/client/screenshots/F10/` (card de/en, notice).

## Slice S3 — status layer UI (code only, no devices)

**What.** Every loading, buffering, seeking, reconnecting and recovering state is visible on every platform, from
the engine signals that exist today (`firstFrame`, `state`, `buffering`, `time`, `error`, `ended`, the new `autoplay`).

| Part | Where |
|---|---|
| `controller.status: PlayerStatus` = `{ spinner, hint: { key, params } \| null, actions }`, derived from the controller state on every read; a 1 s status ticker runs only while something is loading, stalled, recovering or a start state is pending (it drives the hint thresholds and the ladder triggers, then stops). Priority: offline banner → running/pending recovery (countdown, `{time}`) → paused by the system → autoplay blocked → loading after `ready` → stall → muted autoplay | `client/src/player/controller.ts` (`status`, `tickStatus`, `statusTick`) |
| Timeline (state-matrix § 2 c): **after `ready`** spinner at once (the "black at 0:00" is never bare), `H.startSlow` with the cause by 4 s (transcode "converting", remux "preparing", direct "slow connection"), no picture after 20 s direct / 30 s HLS → ladder (T7 `start_timeout`: reload once, then step-down). **Mid-play stall** spinner after 1 s (at once within 2 s of a seek), hint after 4 s (`H.serverSlow` for transcode, else `H.buffering`), 15 s → T5 ladder (lower quality, then step-down); a stall that starts right after a seek reloads at the target first (C09). **Start states** past their budget (queued/resolving/fallback 60 s, planning 30 s, starting 45 s) show `H.startSlow` in the start card (E02) | `controller.ts` constants `SPINNER_MS`, `HINT_MS`, `STALL_LADDER_MS`, `START_BUDGET_MS`, `STATE_BUDGET_MS` |
| `PlayerStatusView`: centred spinner (`pointerEvents: none`) + one glass hint line + action buttons. **TV focus rule:** status never takes or holds focus — on TV it draws no buttons (its actions are on the remote: Play resumes a system pause/blocked autoplay, the Quality panel lowers quality), the whole layer is `pointerEvents: none`; on touch/web the buttons have no preferred focus and the layer is `box-none` so taps elsewhere reach the overlay. Hint lifted above the centre controls while they show | `client/src/screens/player/player-status.tsx` (new) |
| Play screen: status over the picture (not in PiP / end card); start-phase hints (busy countdown, waiting for another device, slow state) inside the start card; hint actions → resume/play, unmute, try now, lower quality, other version/audio/subtitles, cancel/back | `client/src/screens/player/play-screen.tsx` |
| Failure card: the specific hint line (e.g. "The file ends at 1:12:04, 9:12 before the end.") and **"What was tried"** (one line per ladder step with the position and the failure title); **Retry** on a playback that ran resumes in place at `lastGoodPosition` without the resume question (`controller.retry()`); panels and the version picker close on failure (`useCloseOnFailure`) | `play-screen.tsx`, `player-status.tsx` (`RecoveryLog`), `screens/player/use-close-on-failure.ts` (new) |
| Paused by the system (native engines only — web already reports `userPlayback`): an engine `paused` the app did not ask for, held 1 s, is adopted (`paused`, `systemPaused`, progress report) → `H.pausedBySystem` ("Paused: outside the app.") with Resume; a system resume is adopted back. The cause-specific texts (call, headphones, …) need the S6 native probes | `controller.ts` (`watchSystemPause`, `onEngineState`) |
| Autoplay: the web engine emits `autoplay: muted\|blocked`; muted → `H.mutedAutoplay` + Unmute (`controller.unmute()`); blocked → `paused = true`, `H.autoplayBlocked` + Play | `engines/types.ts`, `engines/web-engine.web.tsx`, `controller.ts` |
| Offline banner (`H.offline`) at once from NetInfo (`ControllerOptions.network`, NetInfo by default); the engine stays | `controller.ts` (`onNetwork`) |
| BACKLOG (F8 V2): **Mobile Safari first tap** on hidden controls only shows them — `webSurfaceTap(visible, pointerType)`: a mouse click toggles, a touch on hidden controls shows; pointer moves show the controls only for a mouse (Mobile Safari sends pointer events for touches). **Leaving the system full screen paused** (or any pause from outside the controls) shows the overlay (render-time state adjustment on `controller.paused`) | `client/src/player/surface-tap.ts` (new), `screens/player/player-overlay.tsx` |
| Up-next countdown stops while paused (E07, BACKLOG Player 6); notices stay 8 s on TV (E11) | `screens/player/up-next.tsx`, `overlay-labels.ts` (`noticeMs`) |
| Step-down notice says why (E14): "Switched to transcoding to keep playing. The picture stayed black." (reason = failure code → `notice.because.{start_timeout,T5,T6,T7}`) | `overlay-labels.ts` (`stepDownReasonKey`), `player.{en,de}.json` |
| Texts (de/en, appended): hints `buffering`, `serverError`, `reloading`, cause `outside`; `tried.*` ("What was tried"); `notice.because.*`; codes `playback_stalled` (T5), `start_timeout` (T7), `seek_stalled` (T5) | `client/src/i18n/locales/*.json`, `api/error-codes.ts`, `api/error-categories.ts` |

**Kept (F8 behaviours):** Q1-25 clock plumbing untouched (`usePlayerClock`; the status ticker re-renders 1/s only
while loading/stalled/recovering); Q1-46 `useFadedOut` untouched; Safari start detection (first `timeupdate`) is the
web engine's `firstFrame`, which the status uses; double Back unchanged (`overlayBack`); the "autoplay while paused →
pause" guard moved into `onEngineState` unchanged; `userPlayback` adoption unchanged.

**Decision (E03):** the design asked to keep the start card with a 6th step "Loading picture"; the user's S3 text asks
for a spinner within 1 s and a cause hint by 4 s. Built: the overlay appears at `ready` (Close/Back in reach) with the
centred spinner at once and the hint at 4 s, so there is never a bare black 0:00. A 6th stepper step was not added.

## Slice S4 — recovery ladder, position and progress (code only)

| Part | Where |
|---|---|
| `nextStep(incident, classified, context)` — pure: T1 R with backoff 2/4/8/16 s (offline → wait for `online`), then N, then card; TLS/mixed content → card at once. T2 N once. T3 card. T4 R/N after `Retry-After` or 5/10/20 s, 3 tries; `insufficient_disk` → card. T5 Q (one step, max 2), then S; a seek stall reloads once first. T6 R after `Retry-After` or 5/15 s, 1 N (2 when starting), 1 S, card. T7 R once **per revision**, then S (card when the server answers `no_more_methods`). T8 R once, V if suggested, card. T9 card; `too_many_streams` polls every 10 s for 2 min. T11 R once, N once (`invalid_*` → card). `Incident` keeps the attempts; budgets reset after 2 min without a new failure. `cardActions()` per category after the server's own actions | `client/src/player/recovery/ladder.ts` (new) |
| Controller: **every** failure goes through `handleFailure` (engine errors via `classify`, API errors, failed server states, watchdog-like timers); one incident at a time; a pending (not yet running) step absorbs repeated errors. Steps: **R** `attach(sameSource, position)`; **N** `newStart` = stop the old playback, `POST /playback` with the same release, the current audio/subtitle and preferences at `startPositionTicks = position`, quiet wait (the picture and overlay stay, no stepper), attach, `start` report for the new id; at start (no source yet) N re-runs the start flow; **Q** `/switch { maxHeight: one step down }`; **S** the existing step-down; **G** card with category, actions, tried log, hint | `controller.ts` (`handleFailure`, `runRecovery`, `executeStep`, `newStart`, `giveUp`, `lowerHeight`) |
| Network-class failures never step down (T1 → R/N/card); `engine error → stepDown` is gone; a step-down whose `/switch` fails on the network goes to the T1 ladder (B21) | `controller.ts` |
| User switches: a refused (`T9`/`T11`, e.g. `400 unknown_audio_stream`) or offline (`T1`) `/switch` keeps the running source and shows the notice (no stop/start); other failures keep today's restore | `controller.ts` (`serverSwitch`) |
| Position: every step resumes at `resumePosition` (`lastGoodPosition` while the engine is not steady); `report()` and `stop()` default to `resumePosition`, so heartbeats during an incident never report an engine that reset to 0 | `controller.ts` |
| Early end (C32, D26): an `ended` more than 3 s before the duration of a source that showed a picture (not during a source change / before the start position) → T8: reload once at the position; ending again → card `end_of_stream` with "The file ends at {time}, {missing} before the end." An engine error within 12 s (two segments) of the end finishes playback instead (C13); a `416` on direct play is T8 (C02) | `controller.ts` (`onEnded`, `onEngineError`), `recovery/classify.ts` |
| Server restart / expired session mid-play: Exo/AVPlayer `404`/`410` → T2 → silent N at the position with `H.restarting`; foreground (`AppState active`) and back-online revalidate `GET /playback/{id}`; `404/410` → N (A09, B24); a resume prompt left open ≥ 60 s checks the playback before attaching and starts anew at the choice (E17) | `controller.ts` (`revalidate`, `alive`, `begin`) |
| Start robustness: a failed poll is retried for 30 s (network/429/5xx) before the start fails (B15) | `client/src/player/playback-api.ts` (`waitForPlayback`, `getPlayback`) |
| hls.js media errors: `recoverMediaError` → `swapAudioCodec` + `recoverMediaError` → hand the error to the ladder (T7: reload, then step-down); a new round after 30 s or a new source | `engines/web-engine.web.tsx` (`recoverMedia`) |
| Progress across recoveries: reports carry the new `playbackId` after N, the old one is stopped; `401/403` reports are kept per account without a retry loop until that account's next flush (sign-in), entries older than 24 h are dropped (B23) | `controller.ts`, `client/src/player/progress-queue.ts` |
| Race found by review, fixed failing-first: an error of the old source while a new start (N) runs gave up on the playback (card over a playing restart). Engine errors are ignored while an N step replaces the source (`stepping`); R attaches synchronously, so errors after it belong to the new source | `controller.ts` (`stepping`); test B25 "a late error of the replaced source …" |
| Offline (A24/A25): a failure while offline waits (`H.offline`, engine kept); `online` runs the waiting step at once; offline > 2 min → card; a T1 card retries on its own when the network returns | `controller.ts` (`onNetwork`, `statusTick`) |

**Decisions.**
- **A** (audio fallback) and **V** (other version) as automatic steps: A is not built (needs the server flag); V runs only
  with a server-suggested release, which the playback answer does not carry yet — the card's "Other version" covers it.
- C01 (direct-play capability expired) uses **N** (same release at the position) instead of `/switch` without
  `stepDown`: same effect, no dependency on the server re-resolving inside `/switch`.
- System-pause adoption waits 1 s and requires a shown picture, a steady `paused` engine state, no recovery and no
  own pause — guards against ExoPlayer's `isPlaying=false` during buffering/seeks (to verify live, S9).
- Budgets reset 2 min after the last attempt (approximation of "2 min healthy playback").
- hls.js engine errors still carry no HTTP status (S5), so a hls.js playlist `404` is T1 today (reload with backoff,
  then N after 4 tries) — C10 is closed for ExoPlayer/AVPlayer only (partly, S5 pending).

## Slice S5 — one health watchdog for every engine + web probes (code only, no devices)

**What.** A pure, engine-agnostic watchdog that judges every playing second from the clock, the frame counter,
the audio counter, the stall state and visibility, plus the web probe that feeds it. Verdicts go through the same
`handleFailure({category, code})` ladder as the S4 timers.

| Part | Where |
|---|---|
| `EngineHealth` (frames presented/dropped/decoded, audio progress, readyForDisplay, has video/audio, bandwidth, external, native clock, luma) and `HealthProbe` — the one shape every probe returns (web now, expo-video S6, VLC S7) | `client/src/player/health/types.ts` (new); `PlayerEngine.readHealth?()` in `engines/types.ts` |
| `Watchdog.observe(sample, context)` → `{ verdict, since, level, evidence }`. Verdicts: **clock-frozen** (engine says playing, no stall, neither the event clock nor the engine clock moves for 4 s), **picture-black** (clock ran 3 s, no frame ever presented / readyForDisplay false / pure-black pixels for 20 s from the load), **picture-frozen** (frames moved, then stood while the clock ran 3 s — or 3 frame intervals for low-fps content), **audio-silent** (audio counter stood while the clock ran 3 s), **slideshow** (> 30 % dropped over 10 s = hint, > 60 % = ladder). Every verdict must hold two consecutive ticks; picture/audio verdicts escalate 3 s after they were first seen. Guards: not wanting playback (paused, ended, recovering, offline, before the first picture), settling (2 s after a load, seek or audio/subtitle switch; a seek counts until the clock passed the target by 0.25 s), hidden/backgrounded (PiP keeps clock rules only), engine-announced stall, external playback, audio-only (no picture rules), unknown counters (no rule, never guessed). Any guard restarts every rule. `lastGoodPosition` = clock where frames last moved | `client/src/player/health/watchdog.ts` (new) |
| Controller: a 1 s health ticker **only for engines with a probe** (web today); verdicts: clock-frozen → the stall timeline (spinner 1 s, hint 4 s, T5 ladder 15 s); picture-black → `H.noPicture` + spinner, picture-frozen → `H.recovering` "…at {time}", audio-silent → `H.noAudio`, slideshow → `H.deviceSlow {percent}` with Lower quality; at the ladder level → `classify({kind: 'watchdog'})` → T7 (`picture_black`/`picture_frozen`/`audio_silent`: reload once per revision at the last good position, then step-down) or T5 (`playback_slideshow`: lower quality, then step-down). Stall hint by cause: `H.slowNet {measured, needed}` when the probe's bandwidth < 1.2 × `mediaInfo.bitrateKbps`, `H.serverSlow` for transcode, else `H.buffering`. A pending seek shows the spinner after 0.5 s; a seek that does not arrive in 4 s becomes a stall flagged "after seek" (reload at the target first). Any stall clears once the clock runs ≥ 1 s past where it began (engines that never send `playing`). D35: when the engine clock runs > 2 s ahead of stalled time events, `resumePosition` (reports, recoveries) uses it | `client/src/player/controller.ts` |
| Engine errors carry `status` (`EngineEvent.error.status`) into `classify` | `engines/types.ts`, `controller.ts` (`onEngineError`) |
| Web probe `WebEngine.readHealth()`: frames from `getVideoPlaybackQuality()` (presented = total − dropped), drops, audio from `webkitAudioDecodedByteCount` (Chrome, Safari), `mozHasAudio` (Firefox: presence only), `readyState ≥ 2` after the first frame, `hls.bandwidthEstimate`, Remote Playback `connected` → external, `currentTime` as native clock, luma (below) | `client/src/player/engines/web-engine.web.tsx` |
| hls.js: fatal errors pass `data.response.code` as `status` (404/410 → T2, 5xx → T6, 504 → T5, 0 → T1; closes C10 for hls.js); non-fatal `bufferStalledError` after the first frame → `buffering` (D04); `fragLoadPolicy` 30 s time to first byte, 2 timeout retries, 6 error retries (C08); the hls.js chunk import is retried twice (1 s, 3 s) before `hlsjs:load` (D06) | `web-engine.web.tsx` (`FRAG_LOAD_POLICY`, `HLS_IMPORT_RETRY_MS`) |
| `<video>` MediaError 2/4 on a plain URL (no hls.js) probes the URL with `HEAD` first and passes its status (404 → T2 new start, 0 → T1) (D09) | `web-engine.web.tsx` (`probeStatus`) |
| Canvas luminance sampler (16×9, brightest pixel, `willReadFrequently`), off for good after a `SecurityError` | `client/src/player/engines/web-luma.ts` (new) |
| Codes with card texts de/en: `picture_black`, `picture_frozen`, `audio_silent`, `playback_slideshow` (T7/T7/T7/T5); `classify({kind:'watchdog'})` maps verdicts to them | `api/error-codes.ts`, `api/error-categories.ts`, `recovery/classify.ts`, `en.json`/`de.json` |

**Decisions.**
- **Probe cost.** The health ticker runs only where an engine has `readHealth` (web now). expo-video and VLC get
  their probes in S6/S7; no extra 1 s JS work on TV until then (Q1-25 tick budget). VLC keeps its own `checkStall`
  until S7 moves it into the watchdog.
- **Frames source on web.** `getVideoPlaybackQuality()` once per tick instead of a permanent
  `requestVideoFrameCallback` loop (one JS callback per frame); rVFC stays for the first-frame detection. On
  **Safari native HLS** rVFC never fires (F8) and the counters are unproven, so frame counts are reported only once
  they were seen moving on that element; before that no picture rule runs there (never guessed). hls.js/MSE counters
  are trusted from the load on.
- **Luminance only for MSE data.** The Dev World media is cross-origin from the web app (Metro :8081 → server :39300,
  no Metro proxy); Dev World answers `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` on `/api/v1/stream` and `/api/v1/transcode`
  when the request has an `Origin` (`DevWorldHttp.cs:20-35`). hls.js loads with XHR (Origin sent, CORS passed) into
  MSE, so its picture is readable; a plain `<video src>` sends no Origin and taints the canvas. I did **not** set
  `crossOrigin="anonymous"` (a production server without CORS headers would then not play at all). The luma rule is
  deliberately narrow: pure black (brightest pixel ≤ 2) from the load on, for 20 s of clock, within the first 60 s —
  dark scenes and fades never qualify.
- **Audio presence.** `webkitAudioDecodedByteCount` counts only once it moved on that element (a counter that stays
  0 under some MSE build must not turn every hls.js playback into "no sound" + step-down). No WebAudio analyser:
  `createMediaElementSource` reroutes the element's audio and cross-origin media plays silence through it — the probe
  would cause the failure it looks for.
- **Black/frozen resume position** is where frames last moved (`watchdog.lastGoodPosition`), not the running clock.
- **Bug found by the D39 test and fixed:** a recovery step that already ran (source reloaded, waiting for a picture)
  blocked the start and stall budgets (`calm` required `!recovery`), so a reload that never showed a picture spun
  "Resuming at …" forever. Only a *pending* step holds the budgets now (`statusTick`).
- Harness: probing is opt-in (`harness.features.probe = true`); scripted engines without it keep the S2–S4 behaviour
  (their clocks stand still between events, which a probe would rightly call frozen).

## Live checks for S9 — S5 rows (web; one browser at a time, Dev World with B12 faults)

Observe in the dev build console: `__streamarrPlayer.status`, `.health`, `.recovery`, `.failure?.tried`; screenshots at
1 s, 5 s, 20 s after the fault fires. Chrome with `--mute-audio` (the audio counter still runs muted).
- **C10 / D01 (Chrome, hls.js, remux title):** mid-play `seg_status {target: media, status: 404, code:
  unknown_transcode, mode: once}` → no card, new start at the position (new `playbackId` in the network tab), no
  step-down notice. Repeat with `status: 503, code: segment_unavailable` → "Problem on the server. Retrying in 5 s…",
  reload; `api_drop`-style transport (DevTools offline) → "Reconnecting…".
- **C21:** `seg_status {target: init, status: 503, code: init_unavailable}` → serverError hint, reload after 5 s.
- **C12 / C18 / D10:** `playlist_endless {segments: 10}` → at the end of segment 10 the clock stands: spinner, hint by
  4 s, after 15 s a step-down notice. Safari macOS / iPhone simulator Safari (native HLS) the same (D10).
- **C16 / C17 / D02:** `seg_truncate {percent: 50}` and `seg_corrupt {mode: box}` on video → spinner, then hls.js
  recover → swap codec → reload at the position → step-down (log the `tried` list).
- **D33 / C28 (black):** no B12 fault renders black with a running clock. (a) Firefox or Chrome without HEVC: play a
  Dev World HEVC title by direct play (`engine` auto) → expect "No picture…", reload, then step-down to transcode;
  (b) in Chrome, after 10 s of playback run in the console
  `const v=document.querySelector('video'); v.getVideoPlaybackQuality=()=>({totalVideoFrames:0,droppedVideoFrames:0})`
  right after a seek → black verdict within ~6 s of clock, then reload.
- **D34 (frozen):** console `const q=v.getVideoPlaybackQuality(); v.getVideoPlaybackQuality=()=>q` mid-play →
  "The picture is stuck. Reloading at {time}…", reload at that time; `seg_corrupt {mode: mdat}` on video may also
  freeze the picture (record which).
- **D36 / C20 / C27 (silent):** remux title with ≥ 2 audio renditions, `split_abort {rendition: <current>,
  afterBytes: 4096}` → "No sound. Reloading the audio…", reload, then step-down; Chrome with an E-AC-3 rendition the
  browser cannot decode (C27) if the Dev World catalog has one.
- **D38 / C29 (slideshow):** Chrome DevTools Performance → CPU 20× slowdown on a 1080p/4K title → "This device can't
  keep up (N % of frames dropped)" with Lower quality; > 60 % lowers the quality by itself.
- **C03 / C08:** `throttle {target: video, kbps: 2000}` on a remux title → after 4 s of stall "Slow connection: 2 of
  N Mbit/s"; `transcode_slow {readrate: 0.5}` → "The server converts slower than playback", then lower quality
  (and no hls.js timeout before 30 s: check the fragment request in the network tab).
- **D29:** `seg_delay {ms: 25000, target: video}` then seek +10 min → spinner within 1 s, hint at ~8 s, reload at the
  target at ~19 s.
- **D06:** DevTools → Network → block `*hls*.js` chunk, start a remux title → two retries (1 s, 3 s) visible, then
  reload/new start, then the card with Retry; unblock → Retry plays.
- **D09 (Safari, direct play):** `playback_gone` on a direct-play playback mid-play → media error → `HEAD` 404 → new
  start at the position.
- **D35:** not provokable live without freezing the whole JS thread (the watchdog would stop too); jest only.

## Code review S1-S4 (orchestrator-requested)

#### Review: F10 S1–S4 — independent code review

Reviewer: unattended subagent · 2026-10-05 · worktree `/Users/til/Development/streamarr-f10r` (round-i/f10 @ 0aceb5c)
Scope: `git diff 4673ce8..HEAD -- client` (F10 S1–S4 + F8 slice 7 merge), focus `player/**`, `screens/player/**`,
`api/error-*.ts`, `player/recovery/**`. No product code was changed; the worktree is byte-clean at the end (`git status` empty).

### Gates (once, before probes/mutations)
| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| `npx tsc --noEmit` | exit 0 |
| `npx expo lint` | exit 0 |
| `npx prettier --check src jest` | clean |
| `npx jest --maxWorkers=2` | 126 suites passed, 1198 passed, 93 todo, 0 failed (60 s) |

### Method
1. Read ladder, classify, hints, controller (1463 lines), status view, play screen, web engine, playback-api and progress-queue diffs, the F10 journal and the state matrix.
2. Wrote **throwaway probe tests** on the S2 harness (real controller + ScriptedEngine + FakeServer) for every suspected path, ran them,
   then deleted them from the worktree (copy in the appendix). P = probe id below. "confirmed" = the probe reproduces it.
3. 24 mutations through a script (apply → `jest src/player src/screens/player src/api src/components` → restore bytes).

### Findings

| # | Severity | file:line | What is wrong | Failure scenario (matrix row → what the viewer sees) | Suggested fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **bug** (confirmed P1) | `player/controller.ts:543-544`, `:892` | After an R/N/Q/S step the recovery stays `running` until the first picture, and `statusTick`'s `calm` needs `!this.recovery`. So the start budget and the stall ladder never fire while a recovery runs, and nothing gives up. | D-/C-rows with a reload (T7 `decode_error`, T6, T1, T8 …): if the reloaded source never shows a frame and never errors (AVPlayer waiting forever, a black hls.js start, VLC silent), the viewer sees **"Playback stopped. Resuming at 1:40…" with a spinner forever**. Probe: 10 min later it is still `phase: playing`, `hint: reloading`, no card. The same happens when the viewer is paused during the reload and the engine sends no `firstFrame` while paused. | Give a running step its own budget: reuse `loadingSince`/`startBudget()` while `recovery.running` (drop `!this.recovery` from `calm` for the start-budget branch when the recovery is running), or start a per-step deadline in `executeStep` that sends `T7 start_timeout` into `handleFailure`. |
| 2 | **bug** (confirmed P2) | `controller.ts:997` (`stepDown` returns early when `stepDownRevision === revision`), `:534`, `ladder.ts:145-150` | The S step can be a no-op: `stepDown` returns silently on a revision it already stepped down from, and `executeStep` cannot tell. The recovery stays "running" with no new source, so finding 1 makes it endless. T7's R budget is per revision, but S has no budget at all, and T5's S has none either. | D41/B11 path: decode error → R → decode error → S → `/switch` answers `500` → T6 R after 5 s → decode error → T7 (R already used on this revision) → S → **no-op** → **"No picture. Trying another way to play…" plus a spinner forever** over the broken picture (probe: still there after 10 min, 1 switch sent). The T5 stall → S → switch fails on the network → T1 → R → stall → S path behaves the same. | Have `stepDown` return whether it ran. In `executeStep`, a no-op S → `giveUp`. In the ladder, count S per revision like R (`incident.count(['S'], undefined, revision)`), so a second S on the same revision is G. |
| 3 | **bug** (confirmed P3) | `controller.ts:346-357` (`onNetwork`), `:901` | `stallSince` keeps running while offline. When the network returns, `calm` is true at once and the stall is already ≥ 15 s old, so the next tick sends **T5 → Q or S**. A network outage turns into a lower quality or a step-down, which breaks "network-class never steps down". | A24/A25: Wi-Fi gone for 30 s mid-play while the engine buffers without an error (AVPlayer waits, hls.js still retrying). Wi-Fi returns → within 1 s `/switch {stepDown: true}` (probe) → "Switched to transcoding…" notice and a worse method for the rest of the film. | On `online` (and on going offline) restart the stall clock: `if (this.stallSince) this.stallSince = Date.now()`. Better still, do not count offline time against the stall budget. |

An in-engine audio pick (`selectAudio` local path, `:1200-1205`) or subtitle pick (`:1374-1378`) never reaches `playback.mediaInfo`, so every R drops it. (N and Q pass `currentAudio()/currentSubtitle()`, which also falls back to the server's pick once the failed engine has cleared its track list.) | Viewer switches to German audio and German subtitles (in-engine), then any reload (T1/T6/T7/T8/T11 R, start timeout) → **English audio, subtitles off**, with no hint (probe: `currentAudio()` 1 instead of 2, `currentSubtitle()` null instead of 4). Breaks "selected audio/subtitle survive every recovery". | Capture `currentAudio()`/`currentSubtitle()` into the `Recovery` when it is created (while the engine still lists tracks). Pass them to `attach` → `applyServerTracks` (as overrides) and to `newStart`/Q. Add a row test for each step. |

### Tests — do the row tests guard their rows?
Mostly yes for the decisions they assert. They are **happy-path shaped** for the hard rows, though:
- C32 only scripts `started()` + `time()` between the two ends (finding 5 slips through).
- B25 only covers a start that succeeds at once (finding 6).
- No row reloads with an in-engine track pick (finding 4), lets a user switch race a pending step (finding 7), lets a reloaded source stay silent (findings 1, 2), or stalls across an offline period (finding 3).
- The ScriptedEngine keeps `position` on `error`, so "resume at `resumePosition`, not the engine's reset 0" is untested (M04).
- The timing tests advance by the **imported constants** (`HINT_MS`, `START_BUDGET_MS`, `STALL_LADDER_MS`), so a wrong value stays green (M11). Assert the literal 1 s / 4 s / 15 s / 20 s / 30 s from the design at least once.
- E12 "no preferred focus" asserts `not.toHaveProp('hasTVPreferredFocus', true)` on the testID node, which does not carry the prop, so M15 survives.

**14 of 24 caught, 10 survived** (M01, M04, M05, M11, M13, M15, M16, M17, M18, M22). The journal's 34-mutation proof is accurate for
its own mutations. The survivors are in budgets, position, progress and timing, which the requirement names explicitly.

### Platform notes
- **TV**: the status layer is `pointerEvents: none` with no buttons, so it cannot take focus (E12 holds; M14 caught). The hint actions are not reachable
  on TV (`tryNow`, `otherAudio` …). The journal's claim that the remote/panels cover them holds except for "Try now" (TV waits for the timer).
- **Web/Safari**: the first tap is fine (M09). The pointer-move filter now shows the controls only for a mouse. hls.js media-error budget OK within the 30 s window (finding 18).
  Autoplay hints have resolution gaps (findings 8, 9).
- **Native**: system-pause adoption needs live verification (ExoPlayer `isPlaying=false` during seeks); not reviewable without devices.
- **i18n**: en/de key sets identical (870 / 145 keys). Every hint, cause, action, `tried.*`, `notice.because.*` and `errors.categories.*` key exists in both.
  ICU placeholder mismatches exist only in older keys (`upNext.countdown` and settings), none in F10 keys.

### Summary
S1–S4 are a solid base: the taxonomy and unknown-code texts are right, the ladder decision is pure and readable, and the gates are green.
**The executor around the ladder is not yet "every state ends explained":** probes reproduce endless spinners when a recovered source stays silent (1) or a
step-down is a no-op (2); an offline period that ends in a step-down (3); lost audio/subtitle picks on every reload (4); the C32 frozen frame
when the repeat end comes first (5); the B25 server restart giving up on a single transient `503` (6); and a user switch raced by a stale recovery (7).
Fix 1, 2, 4 and 6 first: they hit the user's requirement directly. Add the probe scenarios (appendix) as row tests, and tighten the 10 surviving mutations.

### Appendix — probe tests (deleted from the worktree; put in `client/src/player/__tests__/` to reproduce)
Expected results on 0aceb5c: P1, P2, P3, P4, P5, P6, P8, P10 fail (= the defects above). P9, P13 and P14 log the behaviour.

```ts
import { harness, reply } from '@/../jest/player/harness';
import { fakeNetwork, playing, settle, starts } from '@/../jest/player/play';

jest.mock('@/player/engines', () => jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness').enginesModule());

beforeEach(() => {
  harness.reset();
  jest.useFakeTimers();
});
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

const decode = 'MediaCodecVideoRenderer error: decoder init failed';
const exo = (status: number) => `Source error: InvalidResponseCodeException: Response code: ${status}`;
const minutes = (n: number) => jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(n * 60_000);

it('P1 a reload whose source never shows a picture (no error) spins forever', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
  harness.engine.fail(decode);
  await settle();
  expect(harness.engine.sources).toHaveLength(2);
  await minutes(10);
  console.log('P1', c.phase, JSON.stringify(c.status), c.failure?.code);
  expect(c.phase).toBe('failed');
});

it('P2 a second step-down on the same revision is a no-op: spinner forever', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
  harness.engine.fail(decode);
  await settle();
  harness.server.answer('switch', reply.error(500, 'server_error'));
  harness.engine.fail(decode);
  await settle();
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
  harness.engine.fail(decode);
  await settle();
  await minutes(10);
  console.log('P2', c.phase, JSON.stringify(c.status), c.failure?.code, harness.server.sent('switch').length);
  expect(c.phase).toBe('failed');
});

it('P3 a stall while offline steps quality/method down the moment the network returns', async () => {
  const network = fakeNetwork();
  const c = await playing({ network }, {}, 100);
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'buffering', buffering: true });
  network.set(false);
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000);
  network.set(true);
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
  console.log('P3 switches', JSON.stringify(harness.server.sent('switch').map((r) => r.body)));
  expect(harness.server.sent('switch')).toHaveLength(0);
  await c.stop();
});

it('P4 an in-engine audio pick is reverted by a reload (R)', async () => {
  const audioTracks = [
    { index: 1, language: 'en', deliveredAs: 'original', selected: true },
    { index: 2, language: 'de', deliveredAs: 'original', selected: false },
  ];
  const c = await playing({}, { mediaInfo: { durationTicks: 6e9, audioTracks, subtitleTracks: [] } } as never, 100);
  const tracks = (sel: number) => ({
    audio: [
      { id: 'a0', label: 'en', language: 'en', selected: sel === 0 },
      { id: 'a1', label: 'de', language: 'de', selected: sel === 1 },
    ],
    subtitles: [],
  });
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: tracks(0) } as never);
  await c.selectAudio(audioTracks[1] as never);
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: tracks(1) } as never);
  harness.engine.time(120);
  harness.engine.fail(decode);
  await settle();
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: tracks(0) } as never);
  const audio = harness.engine.commands.filter((x) => x.startsWith('audio:'));
  console.log('P4 audio commands', audio, 'currentAudio', c.currentAudio());
  expect(c.currentAudio()).toBe(2);
  await c.stop();
});

it('P5 an early end that repeats right after the reload is swallowed', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 0);
  harness.engine.time(300, 600);
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'ended' });
  await settle();
  expect(harness.engine.source?.startPosition).toBe(300);
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'firstFrame' });
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'ended' });
  await minutes(5);
  console.log('P5', c.phase, c.ended, c.failure?.code, JSON.stringify(c.status));
  expect(c.failure?.code).toBe('end_of_stream');
});

it('P6 server restart: new start refused while warming up reloads the dead source, then gives up', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
  harness.server.answer('start', reply.error(503, 'server_starting'));
  harness.engine.fail(exo(404));
  await settle();
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
  harness.engine.fail(exo(404));
  await settle();
  console.log('P6', c.phase, c.failure?.code, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried?.map((a) => a.step + a.category)));
  expect(c.phase).not.toBe('failed');
});

it('P8 a user switch during a pending reload is reloaded again at the stale position', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
  harness.engine.fail(exo(503));
  await settle();
  harness.engine.time(100);
  const ok = await c.setQuality(720);
  harness.engine.started();
  harness.engine.time(130);
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
  console.log('P8', ok, harness.engine.sources.map((s) => s.startPosition), JSON.stringify(c.status));
  expect(harness.engine.sources).toHaveLength(2);
  await c.stop();
});

it('P9 offline at start: no offline hint, no wait for the network', async () => {
  const network = fakeNetwork();
  harness.server.answer('start', reply.offline(), reply.offline(), reply.offline(), reply.offline(), reply.offline(), reply.offline(), reply.offline());
  const { newController } = jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness');
  const c = newController({ network });
  network.set(false);
  await c.start();
  console.log('P9 status', JSON.stringify(c.status), c.offline);
  await minutes(2);
  console.log('P9 after', c.phase, c.failure?.code, starts().length);
  network.set(true);
  await settle();
  console.log('P9 online', c.phase, starts().length);
});

it('P10 an in-engine subtitle pick is reverted by a reload (R)', async () => {
  const subtitleTracks = [
    { index: 3, language: 'en', deliveredAs: 'webvtt', selected: false },
    { index: 4, language: 'de', deliveredAs: 'webvtt', selected: false },
  ];
  const c = await playing({}, { mediaInfo: { durationTicks: 6e9, audioTracks: [], subtitleTracks } } as never, 100);
  const tracks = (sel: number) => ({
    audio: [],
    subtitles: [
      { id: 's0', label: 'en', language: 'en', selected: sel === 0 },
      { id: 's1', label: 'de', language: 'de', selected: sel === 1 },
    ],
  });
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: tracks(-1) } as never);
  await c.selectSubtitle(subtitleTracks[1] as never);
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: tracks(1) } as never);
  harness.engine.fail(decode);
  await settle();
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: tracks(-1) } as never);
  console.log('P10 subtitle commands', harness.engine.commands.filter((x) => x.startsWith('subtitle')), c.currentSubtitle());
  expect(c.currentSubtitle()).toBe(4);
});

it('P13 blocked autoplay hint stays while the video plays from outside the controls', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 0);
  harness.engine.emit({ type: 'autoplay', result: 'blocked' });
  harness.engine.time(5);
  harness.engine.time(10);
  console.log('P13', JSON.stringify(c.status), c.paused);
});

it('P14 hint during a running N start says "Retrying in 0 s"', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
  harness.server.answer('start', reply.ok(harness.server.playback({ state: 'starting', pollAfterMs: 1000 } as never)));
  harness.server.answer('poll', reply.ok(harness.server.playback({ state: 'starting', pollAfterMs: 1000 } as never)), reply.ok(harness.server.playback({ state: 'starting', pollAfterMs: 1000 } as never)));
  harness.engine.fail('Source error: java.net.UnknownHostException: Unable to resolve host');
  await settle();
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(2_000);
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(20_000);
  harness.engine.fail('Source error: java.net.UnknownHostException: Unable to resolve host');
  await settle();
  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10_000);
  console.log('P14', JSON.stringify(c.status), JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried?.map((a) => a.step)));
  await c.stop();
});
```

## Slice S4b — fixes for the code review S1-S4 (code only, no devices)

Worked 2026-10-05 02:37–(see verification) in `../streamarr-f10` on top of the S5 working tree (S5 not committed);
nothing committed. Every finding was fixed at the root; each reviewer probe became a permanent row test, and each
fix is proven by a mutation that reverts exactly that fix and makes its row test fail (table below). That is the
"fails before the fix" proof: the fixes sit on uncommitted S5 code, so the tests could not be run against 0aceb5c
itself without a stash (forbidden); the reviewer's own probe run on 0aceb5c is the second half of the proof.

**Structure.** The ladder *execution* moved out of the controller:

| Module | What it owns |
|---|---|
| `client/src/player/recovery/runner.ts` (new) | `RecoveryRunner`: the incident, the one pending or running step, its timers, the absorption of repeats while a step waits, the per-step budget (90 s for the server/source work, aborts the step's requests), no-op steps (complete → card), the dead-source flag after a new start, `cancel()` for the viewer's own switches, `reset()` for the card's Retry, `online()`/`runNow()` |
| `client/src/player/recovery/status.ts` (new) | `statusOf(input)`: the pure spinner/hint/actions derivation (§ 2 c) and `runningHint` |
| `client/src/player/health/monitor.ts` (new) | `HealthMonitor`: the watchdog's timer, probe reads, native clock and findings (moved from the S5 controller code) |
| `client/src/player/recovery/other-version.ts` (new) | ladder step **V**: the versions API with this device's hints, best other playable version by rank, never one already played |
| `client/src/player/controller.ts` | wiring only for recovery: `runStep` (R/N/Q/S/V actions), `failureOf`, `giveUp`; one `startRequest()` builder replaces three copies of the start body |

**Fixes** (review finding → root cause → fix → row test):

| # | Finding | Root cause | Fix | Row test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Endless "Resuming at …" after a reload that stays black (P1) | a running step held the start/stall budgets (`calm` needed no recovery); paused, nothing ran either | budgets only wait for a *pending* step; a running step has the start budget from its attach (20/30 s → next step), the server/source work has a 90 s step budget; the start budget runs while paused too, and a paused source that reached `paused` counts as loaded; an incident ends on a card after 12 steps (`MAX_ATTEMPTS`), step-downs are capped at 3 per incident | D39 "a reloaded source that never shows a picture …", D39 "… while the viewer has paused …" |
| 2 | Endless "No picture" after a no-op step-down (P2) | `stepDown` returned silently on a revision it had already left; the runner could not tell | `stepDown` returns whether it ran (the per-revision guard is gone; only a running switch blocks); a step that changed nothing completes and ends on the card (and is not listed under "What was tried") | D41 "a second step-down on the same revision …", D41 "a step-down that cannot run …", B06 "no other playable version …" |
| 3 | Back online → immediate T5 step-down (P3) | the stall clock ran on while offline | going offline/online and background/foreground restart the stall, start and seek clocks | A24 "a stall across an offline period …" (also proves the stall ladder still fires 15 s after the return) |
| 4 | In-engine audio/subtitle pick lost on reload (P4, P10) | R re-applied the server's `selected` tracks | the viewer's tracks are captured when the failure happens (`viewerTracks()`), kept through chained steps, re-applied on R (`applyServerTracks(…, viewer)`) and sent with N/Q/S | C07 "… keeps the audio …", C07 "… keeps the subtitle …", C10 "a new start (session gone) keeps the audio and subtitle …" |
| 5 | Short file ends again before a time event → frozen frame (P5) | the repeat was swallowed by the `startFloor`/`pictured` guards | the controller remembers where the source ended early (for the incident's 2 min); an end at the same place (±3 s, the load position when no time event came) is the repeat → T8 → other version → card "The file ends at …" | C32 "a reloaded short file that ends again before any time event …", C32 "playToEnd after a user switch … is not an early end" |
| 6 | Server restart + one 503 on the new start → reload of the dead playback → card (P6) | a failed new start was still decided as `attached` | the runner marks the source dead when an N starts (it stops the old playback); until a source attaches, retries are new starts | B25 "a new start refused once while the server warms up …" |
| 7 | User switch raced by a stale reload; Lower quality during an automatic one (P8) | user switches did not cancel the pending step; no guard while switching | `serverSwitch` without a step context is the viewer's: refused while another switch runs, and it cancels the runner; hint actions are hidden while a step runs and only "Try now"/"Back" are offered while one waits | C14 "a user switch during a pending reload replaces it …", C03 "while the quality goes down on its own …", A23 (actions `[]` while running) |
| 8 | Muted-autoplay hint never cleared via the overlay speaker (review 8) | the overlay muted the engine directly | `controller.setMuted()` is the one path (overlay button, key, hint); the controller owns `muted` (the browser's muted autoplay sets it), the overlay reads it | D07 "muted autoplay: the overlay speaker button …" |

**Risks** (all fixed in code; device checks in "Live checks for S9 — S4b"):

| Review | Fix | Test |
|---|---|---|
| 9 blocked autoplay starts anyway | the first clock movement after a blocked autoplay clears the hint and the pause | D08 (review P13) |
| 10 offline at the start | the controller subscribes to the network in its constructor; an offline start waits with `H.offline` and starts when the network returns; a start-phase restart keeps the viewer's resume choice (`chosenStart`, also review 25) | A24 (review P9) |
| 11 client exceptions on the generic card | `failureOf()` turns any non-API exception into `player_internal_error` (T11, own de/en text, Retry, the code stays visible); `fail()` never stores `unknown` | E09 (review 11) |
| 12 VLC drop read as a short file | an early end while offline is T1; ends at different places are `stream_interrupted` (T1, reconnect, never a step-down); otherwise the server is asked (`GET playback`) and its duration decides: gone → T2, the full length → normal end, else T8 | D26 (two rows) |
| 13 "Retrying in 0 s" while a step runs | running steps show what they do (`reloading`, `restarting`, `lowering`, `steppingDown`, `switchingVersion`, `recovering`, `noPicture`); a waiting countdown never shows less than 1 s | A23 (review P14) |
| 14 flapping network retry loop | an automatic card retry needs 2 s of stable network, at most 3 per 10 minutes, and keeps the incident's budget (only the viewer's Retry resets it) | A25 (review 14) |
| 15 step V decided but not built | built (`other-version.ts`): T8 (after its reload) and `no_more_methods` try the best other version once at the same position, with the viewer's language preferences; notice "Switched to another version to keep playing."; none → card | B06 (two rows), ladder unit tests |
| 16 Sign in filtered off the card | `card-actions.ts` keeps `signIn`; the play screen routes it to `/profiles` (F8's account screen); a 401/refused refresh during a new start or the foreground check ends on that card | A03 (two rows; A03 stays partly open: pausing + local save before the card is S6) |
| 19 a11y chatter | the hint is announced once per hint key (`AccessibilityInfo.announceForAccessibility`), the accessible label is taken at that moment (no per-second re-reads), the spinner is hidden from screen readers while a hint says the same, and has its "Loading" label otherwise; `accessibilityLiveRegion` removed | E11 "screen readers hear a hint once per change …" |
| 18 hls.js media error every ≥ 30 s recovered forever | besides the 30 s window, at most 6 in-engine media recoveries per source; the 7th goes to the ladder | D02 "a media error every 35 s …" |
| 20 hint over the controls | `statusLayout()`: with visible touch/web controls the hint sits under the top bar (below a notice) and the spinner leaves the centre to the Play cluster; hidden controls and TV keep the centred layout | E11 "with visible controls the hint moves …" (layout rule; screenshots in S9) |

Quality: controller.ts 1639 lines (S5 state 1655, before the extraction 1799) — ladder execution, status
derivation and the health monitor live in `recovery/runner.ts`, `recovery/status.ts`, `health/monitor.ts`; review 25
(start-phase restart asked "Resume?" again, three copies of the start body) fixed with `chosenStart` and
`startRequest()`; review 22 (unused hint keys / classify sources) left as the S6/S7 contract.

Also: the C13 end-of-stream margin read the engine position after the error (engines reset it to 0) → it now uses the
last good position (found when the ScriptedEngine started to reset its clock on errors like the real engines);
the 4-line JSDoc is one line; `setOverlayShownState` is `setControlsShown`.

**Harness changes.** `ScriptedEngine` resets its position on `error` (ExoPlayer/AVPlayer/hls.js do) and echoes
`setAudioTrack`/`setSubtitleTrack` as a `tracks` event with the new selection; `FakeServer` has a `versions` route
(default: no versions). Existing rows changed on purpose: A25 (the card retries 2 s after the return), D26 (a short
file ends at the same place again: 20:01 instead of 20:05, and the server's duration matches the file), B11/E09 keep
`tried` = R, S (an empty version search is not an attempt).

## Live checks for S9 — S4b (one device at a time, Dev World with B12 faults)
- **P1/D39 reload stays black:** Google TV (Exo) or iPhone simulator (AVPlayer): `seg_status {target: video, status: 500, code: transcode_failed, mode: once}` then `seg_stall {target: video, seconds: 600}` right after the reload → "Playback stopped. Resuming at …", then within 30 s "Trying another way to play at …"; after at most 12 steps a card with "What was tried", never a spinner beyond ~2 min. Paused variant: pause, then the same faults → no spinner while paused, card or picture after the budget.
- **P2/D41:** Chrome (hls.js): `seg_corrupt {mode: garbage, mode: always}` + `api_status {target: api:switch, status: 500, mode: once}` → reload, step-down fails, reload, second step-down runs or the card; never "No picture" for more than a minute.
- **P3/A24:** Android phone AVD: playing remux, `svc wifi disable` while `throttle {kbps: 50}` keeps it buffering, 30 s later `svc wifi enable` → offline banner, then the stall spinner again; **no** step-down notice within 15 s of the return.
- **P4/P10:** Chrome and iPhone simulator: pick German audio and German subtitles in the panels (in-engine), then `seg_status {status: 500, code: transcode_failed, mode: once}` → after the reload the panels still show German/German, audio audible in German.
- **P5/C32:** `direct_truncate {percent: 50}` on a direct-play title, seek to just before the cut → reload, immediate second end → card "The file ends at …" with Other version (and the versions search in the server log), no frozen frame.
- **P6/B25:** server restart on the dedicated port (39330) mid-play with `api_status {target: api:start, status: 503, mode: once}` armed for the first start after the restart → "The server ended this playback. Restarting at …", "The server is busy. Retrying in 5 s…", then playback at the position; no card.
- **P8/C14:** `seg_status {status: 503, code: segment_unavailable, retryAfter: 10, mode: once}` → during "Retrying in 10 s" pick 720p → the switch plays on; no jump back 10 s later. During an automatic quality step the hint has no buttons.
- **8/D07 (Chrome, autoplay muted by a fresh profile without a gesture):** start a title → "Sound off — tap to unmute."; press the overlay speaker button → sound on, hint gone, the button shows "Mute".
- **9/D08 (Safari macOS, Low Power autoplay policy or `--autoplay-policy=user-gesture-required` in Chrome):** blocked → "The browser blocked autoplay. [Play]"; start with the media key → hint gone, overlay shows Pause.
- **10/A24 start offline:** iPhone simulator with Network Link Conditioner 100 % loss, open a title → start card with "No network connection…"; restore → the start continues without the resume question repeated.
- **11/E09:** dev build with a thrown error injected via `__streamarrPlayer.engine.load = () => { throw new Error('x') }` (dev console) → card "The player hit an internal error" + Retry + "Error code player_internal_error".
- **12/D26 VLC drop:** Android AVD with `engine: vlc`, direct play, `svc wifi disable` at 41:00 → offline banner, no "file ends" card; enable → resumes at 41:00. Second variant: `seg_reset`/`direct` reset twice at different positions → "Connection lost. Retrying…", never "The file ends at".
- **14/A25 flapping:** `svc wifi disable/enable` every second for 20 s while on the "Connection lost" card → no restart until the network is stable for 2 s; at most 3 automatic restarts in 10 minutes.
- **15/B06 other version:** a title with two releases, `transcode_never_start {code: transcode_failed}` + engine pinned so every method fails (`no_more_methods`) → "Trying another version at …", then the second release plays at the same position with the notice "Switched to another version to keep playing.".
- **16/A03:** `session_revoke {reason: admin}` then `playback_gone` → restart refused 401 → card with "Sign in again" → opens the account screen.
- **19 (TalkBack on the Android phone AVD, VoiceOver on the iPhone simulator):** `api_status {target: api:start, status: 503, retryAfter: 10}` → the busy hint is announced once, not every second; the spinner is not read separately.
- **20 layout (screenshots phone landscape 390 pt tall, iPad portrait, web 1280, Google TV):** a stall with the controls shown → the hint sits under the top bar (under a notice when one shows), the Play cluster stays free; controls hidden → centred spinner + hint; TV unchanged (centre, no buttons).

## Slice S4c — B13 adoption (code only, no devices)

Worked 2026-10-05 03:25–03:45 in `../streamarr-f10` on top of 56b3847 + the main merge (B12, B13, R1). Nothing
committed. B13 read from `docs/client/journal/B13.md` "Summary" and the types in `client/src/api/schema.d.ts`.

| B13 part | Client change | Where | Rows / tests |
|---|---|---|---|
| (2) `Retry-After` on 503/504 (`segment_timeout`, `segment_evicted`, …) | **hls.js retries:** the engine wraps hls.js's own loader (`retryAfterLoader`): every failed answer's `Retry-After` is noted before hls.js computes its retry delay; the fragment policy's `retryDelayMs`/`maxRetryDelayMs` are getters that never go below it (no hint: 1/2/4 s as before). 3 in-engine retries (was 6), then the ladder — **no double loop**: hls.js waits what the server asked, the ladder adds its own budgeted R/N afterwards. **Ladder:** a fatal hls.js error carries `retryAfter` (seconds, from `data.networkDetails`) in `EngineEvent.error`; the controller hands it to the runner, whose T4/T6 wait step already uses `retryAfter` instead of its backoff. **Native engines** (Exo/AVP) expose no headers: their errors keep the 5/15 s T6 backoff, which is never shorter than the server's `Retry-After: 1` (decision; a header probe is S6 work). Playlist retries inside hls.js's loader keep hls.js's 1/2/4 s (≥ 1 s, the server's value) — the loader retries internally before the wrapper sees the error | `engines/hls-retry-after.ts` (new), `engines/web-engine.web.tsx`, `engines/types.ts` | D01 "a fatal 503 with Retry-After hands the wait to the ladder, which reloads after exactly that" (7 s, not 5 s); `engines/__tests__/hls-retry-after.test.ts` (header from XHR/Response, delays 10/10/10 s with `Retry-After: 10`, 1/2/4 s without, loader notes and forgets) |
| (3) `playbackAlive` in the progress answer | `ProgressQueue.onAnswer` gets every answer (also of queued reports sent later). `false` for the **current** playback's id while playing and no recovery runs → ladder T2 `playback_not_found` with `quiet: true`: a new start at the position with the viewer's audio/subtitle (the N step of S4b), **no hint, no notice, no card** while the old picture plays; only the new source's loading spinner shows. If the new start fails, the next failure is not quiet (its hint/card as usual). `true`/`null`, `stop` reports and answers about an older playbackId change nothing. Found on the way: `this.onAnswer?.(await send(...))` skips the *send* when nobody listens (an optional call does not evaluate its arguments) — written as two statements | `progress-queue.ts`, `controller.ts` (`onProgressAnswer`), `recovery/runner.ts` (`FailureExtra.quiet`), `recovery/status.ts` | B25 "a heartbeat answered playbackAlive false: a silent new start …" (position 120, `audioStreamIndex: 2`, status without hint, no notice), B25 "playbackAlive true or null, or false for an older playback, changes nothing", B25 "the silent restart that fails is no longer silent", A09 "the server ended an idle playback …" |
| (4) `/switch audioFallback` | Ladder **step A**: for `audio_decode_error` (new code: Exo `AudioSink`/`AUDIO_TRACK_*`/`MediaCodecAudioRenderer`, Chrome "audio decoder", AVFoundation `AudioToolbox`/`AudioQueue`, hls.js media errors of the **audio** source buffer — the web engine tags them `:audio`) A comes **first** (a reload cannot fix a codec); for `audio_silent` (watchdog) after the one reload per revision. Then the method steps down as before. A runs only while the playback does not convert yet (`PlaybackResponse.audioFallback`) and once per incident. Running hint "No sound. Converting the audio to keep playing at {time}…", notice "Sound converted to stereo to keep playing.", card log "Converted the audio". **Sticky for the viewing:** a new start (N, also the silent `playbackAlive` one) after a conversion switches the new playback to `audioFallback: true` before it attaches | `recovery/ladder.ts` (`'A'`, `AUDIO_CODES`, `LadderContext.audioFallback`), `recovery/classify.ts` (`AUDIO_DECODER`), `controller.ts` (`audioFallback()`, newStart), `recovery/status.ts`, `hints.ts`, texts de/en | D13 ×4 (A first with notice and hint; still failing → one reload → step-down, A asked once; a converting playback skips A; a new start converts again), D02 "hls.js: a codec error of the audio buffer …", C20 (silence: reload, A, reload, step-down), C27 web (silence: reload, A + notice) |

**Rows:** D13 closed; A09, B25, D01, D02, C20 (web), C27 (web) got their B13 tests. D21 (AVPlayer silence heuristic), D25 (VLC
counters) stay with S6/S7 — their verdicts will reach step A through the same `audio_silent` code.

**Changed on purpose:** classify maps Exo `AudioSink…` to `audio_decode_error` (was `decode_error`); C20/C27 expect the
conversion before the step-down; the fake server keeps `audioFallback` sticky like the real one (B13).

## Live checks for S9 — S4c + S8 (Dev World 39300 with B13; one device at a time)
- **Retry-After (Chrome, hls.js):** `seg_status {target: video, status: 503, code: segment_unavailable, retryAfter: 10, mode: {count: 2}}` → network tab: the fragment is re-requested after ~10 s each (not 1/2 s); playback continues without a card. With `mode: always`: after 3 in-engine retries the hint "The server had a problem. Retrying in 10 s…", then the reload. Also `seg_status {status: 504, code: segment_timeout}` → retries ≥ 1 s apart, then "Lower quality".
- **playbackAlive (Chrome and Google TV):** playing remux, `playback_gone {playbackId}` (or a 60 s idle Dev World + pause > 60 s, then play) → within 10 s a new playbackId in the network log / server log, picture goes on at the position, **no** hint, notice or card; the audio track chosen in the panel is still selected. Variant: arm `api_status {target: api:start, status: 503, mode: once}` first → "The server is busy. Retrying in 5 s…" then playback.
- **Audio fallback (Chrome):** a title with E-AC-3/TrueHD audio delivered as remux (browser without decoder) → hls.js audio buffer codec error → "No sound. Converting the audio to keep playing at …", notice "Sound converted to stereo …", server log `/switch audioFallback=true`, audio audible. Google TV / Android phone: `adb shell` force an unsupported passthrough (HDMI audio settings → passthrough "always" without AVR) → Exo `AudioSink` error → same path. Watchdog variant (Chrome): `split_abort {rendition, afterBytes: 2000}` → "No sound", reload, conversion.
- **Subtitles (Chrome):** German WebVTT selected, `subtitle_status {rendition: <index>, status: 404}` → notice "Subtitles “German” couldn’t be loaded and are off. They’ll be tried again in a minute.", picture and audio unaffected, no step-down; clear the fault → after 60 s German subtitles are back. `subtitle_corrupt {mode: header}` → same notice (`subtitle_unreadable`). Safari native and Exo: note what happens (no subtitle event there yet → S6).
- **C24 (Android phone, VLC available):** select a PGS subtitle on a transcode that cannot burn it in (server `deliveredAs: none`) → notice "… can’t be shown here — VLC can show them."; Chrome: the variant without VLC.
- **C31:** a release whose file is empty / `direct_truncate {percent: 0}` → "This version has nothing to play" card after a reload and a version search (server log: versions request); never the end card or up-next.
- **D31 (Chrome, transcode):** press → five times quickly → one segment request burst at the final target (server log: one ffmpeg restart), the time label jumps at once.

### 2.–5. Findings

Severity: **bug** = probe-confirmed wrong state for the viewer; **risk** = plausible or platform-dependent; **test-gap**
= surviving mutation / missing row; **quality** = project rules. Probe ids → appendix.

| # | Severity | file:line | What is wrong | Failure scenario (matrix row → what the viewer sees) | Suggested fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | **bug** (N1) | `player/health/watchdog.ts:136-137`, `:165-171` | The luma "no picture" rule is anchored to the watchdog **baseline**, which every guard resets (seek, pause, buffering, settle, hidden tab). "Pure black from the load on, first 60 s" therefore means "from the last seek/resume on". It also ignores that frames and audio are moving. | D33 false positive: seek (or resume) into ≥ 20 s of pure black with sound (overtures, e.g. *2001* (≈ 3 min black), black openings, gaps before credits; the 16×9 bilinear downscale drops thin credit text) on hls.js → "No picture" + spinner at ~21 s, reload at ~24 s, then a **step-down of a healthy playback**. Probe: hints `[noPicture, reloading]`, 2 loads, reload at 1025 s. | Anchor `darkSince`/the 60 s window to the source load (monitor gets `reset('load')` from `attach` vs `reset('seek')`), and only apply the luma rule while frames do **not** move or are unknown (moving frames + moving audio = black content). Sample luma only while the rule can still fire (cost, R11). |
| B2 | **bug** (N2; platform half to verify live) | `watchdog.ts:90-96` | `external === true` turns off the picture rules only; the audio rule (and slideshow) still run. The design (§ 2 a guards) says "external === true (AirPlay: clock rules only)". While Remote Playback/AirPlay is connected the local element stops decoding, so `webkitAudioDecodedByteCount` stands. | A18/A20 web (Safari AirPlay, Chrome global media controls → Cast use Remote Playback): the TV plays fine, the phone/laptop shows "No sound", then **reloads the local engine** (breaking the cast) and steps down. Probe: hints `[noAudio, reloading]`, 2 loads. | `const local = health.external !== true;` gate `audio()` and `slideshow()` with it, as the design says. |
| B3 | **bug** (N4, X1) | `controller.ts:1065-1070` (stall ladder), `health/monitor.ts:102-111` (stall/escalate) | No end margin outside `onEngineError` (C13 has `END_MARGIN_SECONDS`). A stall, a frozen clock or a picture/audio verdict in the last seconds enters T5/T7. | C13/C32 neighbourhood: hls.js VOD end stall (buffered end < duration, no `ended`), a transcode whose last segment is short, a video track that ends 5–10 s before the audio → "Lowering the quality…"/"Trying another way to play" at 1:59:56, a `/switch {stepDown}` at the end of the film, then possibly a card instead of the end card/up-next. Probes: N4 `/switch {stepDown:true, position 596 s}` + notice `stepDown`; X1 hint `recovering`, reload at 592 s. | One `nearEnd()` (pictured and `position ≥ duration − END_MARGIN_SECONDS`) checked in `statusTick` (stall, seek stall), `monitor.stall`/`escalate` → `finish()`. |
| B4 | **bug** (N5) | `recovery/runner.ts:98-99`, `controller.ts:1595-1608` | A pending step keeps the position (and tracks) captured at the failure; `seekTo` (and in-engine audio/subtitle picks) do not update or cancel it. The S4b fix 7 covers `serverSwitch` only. | C14/A23/B05: "Retrying in 5 s" (T6/T4/T1 backoff up to 16 s or Retry-After) → viewer scrubs to 6:40 → the reload starts at **1:40**: the picture jumps back. Probe: sources `[start, 100]` after `seekTo(400)`. | `seekTo` → `runner.reposition(clamped)` when `current && !current.running` (same for `selectAudio`/`selectSubtitle` local paths → update `current.tracks`). Row test in C14. |
| B5 | **bug** (N6) — regression of old #6 | `recovery/runner.ts:200-201`, `controller.ts:587-602`, `:605-631` | V runs `newStart`, which **stops the old playback**, but only `N` sets `sourceDead` and `replacing`. A transient failure of V's start (T4/T1/T6) is decided as `attached` → **R reloads the stopped playback**; V counts as used, so its release is never tried again; old-source errors during V are not ignored either. | B06/C32 + B25 mix: "The file ends at 5:00" → "Trying another version…" → the server answers 503 once → "The server is busy. Retrying in 5 s" → reload of the stopped p1 (404 on a real server → T2 → N with the **old** release `r1`) → the short file again → card "Video ends early" although `r2` exists. Probe: sources `[p1, p1, p1]`, starts `[_, r2]`, `stop p1`. | `if (step === 'N' \|\| step === 'V')` for `sourceDead` and `replacing`; keep the chosen `releaseId` in the `Recovery` so a chained N after a failed V starts that release. |
| B6 | **bug** (N8 + server routing by reading) | `engines/web-engine.web.tsx:192-197`, `recovery/classify.ts:49-55`; server `Transcoding/TranscodeStreamController.cs:18-86` | The D09 HEAD probe runs for every non-hls.js source, i.e. also **Safari native HLS** (`master.m3u8`). The transcode routes are `[HttpGet]` only (the direct-play route needed an explicit `[HttpHead]`, `Controllers/StreamController.cs:18`), so ASP.NET answers **405**. `mediaStatus(405)` → T11 `http_405`. | D09/C-rows on Safari (macOS, iPhone/iPad web): MediaError 4 (format Safari cannot play) → T11 R → N → card **"The app hit an unexpected problem — http_405"** (no step-down, no "Other version"); MediaError 2 on a lost session → T11 "reloading" instead of T2 "restarting". Probe: `{reason: media_error_4, status: 405}` → `{T11, http_405}` (expected T7). Probe: no error after 120 s. | `fetch(uri, { method: 'HEAD', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5_000) })` → 0 on timeout. |
