Same for audio: a next source without audio (or a dead counter) → "No sound" + reload. Probe: `framesPresented` 0 (defined) on the new HLS source. | Reset the three in `resetForLoad`. |
| R3 | risk (N3; only if audio-only works exist) | `controller.ts:1011` | `hasVideo: info?.video ? true : health.hasVideoTrack` — a server `video: null` never becomes `false`, and the web probe never reports `false`. Design: "hasVideoTrack === false (audio-only: no picture rules)". | Audio-only file / music video with a cover-less stream on hls.js: `totalVideoFrames` 0 → "No picture" + reload + step-down. Probe: hints `[noPicture, reloading]`. | `hasVideo: info ? !!info.video : health.hasVideoTrack`, or probe `hasVideoTrack: false` when `readyState ≥ 2 && videoWidth === 0`. |
| R4 | risk (N10) | `watchdog.ts:152-161` | The low-fps scaling needs an interval measured **since the last reset**; the first interval after every seek/resume is judged at the 3 s default. | 0.2 fps content: "No picture" flickers ~1 s after each seek; < 1/6 fps (still-image talks, VFR static shots, slideshows): **reload/step-down**. Probe: `picture-black/hint` at 4–5 s, `ladder` at 6–7 s for one frame per 8 s. | Keep the frame-interval estimate per source in the monitor across resets; before the first frame after a reset use `max(3 s, 3 × last interval)`. |
| R5 | risk (N11) | `recovery/runner.ts:84-85`, `ladder.ts:39` | The incident resets 2 min after the last **attempt**; nothing remembers recurring incidents. | A decoder glitch every 2.5 min: "Reloading at …" every 2.5 min for the whole film, never a step-down, never an explanation that it keeps happening. Probe: 6 loads, 0 switches. | Count incidents per source/code (e.g. 3 within 15 min → start at S, with the step-down reason "keeps failing"). |
| R6 | risk (false negatives; known S6/S7) | `health/monitor.ts:25`, `:39` | Probe-less engines get **no** watchdog (no ticker), while the class comment says "clock rules only". expo-video and VLC → iOS, Android, Google TV, Apple TV have no clock-frozen/black/frozen/silent detection beyond engine events. | D33/D34/D36/C12 on every native target: frozen picture with a "playing" engine and no `buffering` event stays without spinner/hint until S6/S7. | A snapshot-only clock rule (`getSnapshot()` once per second is cheap) for every engine now, or correct the comment and track it as an S6 blocker. |
| R7 | risk | `web-engine.web.tsx:38-45` vs `controller.ts:138` | hls.js `errorRetry` (6 tries, 1–8 s ≈ 31 s) outlasts `STALL_LADDER_MS` (15 s). | C14/C21 on web: a 20 s segment-503 burst → "Loading slowly"/"The server converts slower…" and a **quality step (Q)** at 15 s before hls.js reports the 503 (T6 "Problem on the server, retrying"). | Keep hls.js' error retries under 15 s in total, or classify a stall with an in-flight retry as T6. |
| R8 | risk (design deviation, not journaled) | `recovery/ladder.ts:157-164` | The matrix T7 row says "picture/audio verdicts: seek nudge first → R once → **A (audio only)** → S". Implemented: R → S. | C27/D36 (undecodable E-AC-3 rendition): "No sound" → reload → **method step-down** where switching to the AAC rendition would have kept the method. | Implement A for `audio_silent` (another audio track/rendition) or journal the deviation. |
| R9 | risk | `controller.ts:1207-1210` | A step's `/switch` that ends in a failed playback returns without restoring `phase` (left `switching`); `serverSwitch` now refuses user switches while `switching`. | B06/T6 after a failed S: during the following wait (T6 N 5 s) the viewer's quality/version/audio picks silently do nothing (no notice). | Restore `phase` (`this.engine ? 'playing' : 'starting'`) in the `!ready && step` branch. |
| R10 | risk (a11y) | `screens/player/player-status.tsx:54-58`, `:107` | The accessible label is frozen at the first text of a hint key; param changes (countdown, "at {time}", percent) are never re-read. | VoiceOver/TalkBack focus on the hint 4 s later still reads "Retrying in 5 s". | Use the live text as label and announce once per key (as now). |
| R11 | risk (cost) | `web-engine.web.tsx:471` | Luma is drawn and read back every second for the whole hls.js session (4K frame → CPU canvas), though the rule only fires in the first 60 s. | Weak web devices/TV browsers: a GPU→CPU readback per second for nothing. | Read luma only while `sinceLoad < 60 s` and frames are unknown/standing (B1). |
| R12 | risk (known E16 partly) | `screens/player/play-screen.tsx:230` | `code = capsError ?? …` keeps `toAppError(error).code` → `unknown` for a device-caps exception → generic "Something went wrong". | E16: the one remaining generic card. | Map client exceptions there to `player_internal_error` too. |
| R13 | risk | `recovery/runner.ts:46`; X2 | The 90 s step budget also cuts legitimate long server work during a mid-play N (repair, queue), and `newStart` hides the server states (`waitForPlayback(…, () => undefined, …)`). | B-rows mid-play: "Restarting at 1:40…" for 90 s ×3 → card "The server took too long" after **290 s** (X2) while the server may report `repairing 40 %`. | Show the server state in the running hint (`startSlow {cause}` / repair %), and extend the budget while the server reports progress. |
| T1 | test-gap | — | Surviving mutations (table below): K07 slideshow min frames, K08 luma 60 s window, **K10 step budget**, **K15 chained-step tracks**, K28 start clock restart (background/offline), K31 watchdog during a recovery, K35 V running hint, **K36 step timeout → `step_timeout`**. | The step budget and its classification are untested end to end; a chained step re-reading tracks after the engine cleared them is untested. | One row per survivor (e.g. B25 "a new start that never gets ready ends after 90 s with `step_timeout`"; C07 "R → S keeps the captured tracks"). |
| T2 | test-gap | — | No rows for: seek during a pending step (B4), V + transient failure (B5), HEAD on native HLS (B6), external + audio (B2), stall/verdict near the end (B3), luma after a seek (B1), per-source counter proof (R2), HEAD timeout (R1). | — | Add the appendix probes as rows. |
| Q1 | quality | `controller.ts:1173-1176` | 4-line JSDoc on `serverSwitch` (project rule: one-line comments). | — | One line. |
| Q2 | quality | `health/types.ts:6`, `:23`; `recovery/classify.ts:9-10`, `:18-36`, `:142-151`; `hints.ts` | Dead/speculative: `framesDecoded`, `HealthProbe`, watchdog verdicts `starting`/`buffering`, `system`/`exception` sources, `SystemCause`, hint keys `serverDown`/`decoder`/`subtitleFailed`/`signedOut`. | — | Remove until S6/S7 use them (review 22 left them as contract; S6/S7 are not in this diff). |
| Q3 | quality | `recovery/runner.ts:205`, `:211`, `:236` | The abort reason `new Error('step_timeout')` is a second error object beside `STEP_TIMEOUT` (declared after the class); the catch infers the timeout from `aborted && current === recovery`. | — | `abort.abort(STEP_TIMEOUT)` and test `signal.reason === STEP_TIMEOUT`. |
| Q4 | quality | `controller.ts:1039-1081` | "Controller = wiring only for recovery" does not hold for budgets: start/stall/offline budgets live in `statusTick`, which reaches into the runner (`runner.cancel(); this.giveUp(recovery.failure, …, runner.attempts)`). | — | Move the offline give-up into the runner (`runner.expireOffline()`); keep the clocks in one place. |
| Q5 | quality | `controller.ts:1152-1171` | `stepDown` returns `true` even when `serverSwitch` returned `false`; correctness relies on `handle` having replaced `current` meanwhile (implicit coupling). | — | Return `ok \|\| this.runner.current !== <recovery>` explicitly, or let `serverSwitch` throw for steps. |
| Q6 | quality | `health/monitor.ts:25` | Comment claims clock rules for probe-less engines (see R6). | — | Fix the comment. |

### Watchdog review notes (no separate finding)
- Guards checked and correct: paused, ended, offline, recovery pending/running, `phase !== 'playing'`, before the first
  picture, settle 2 s after load/seek/track switch (+ until the clock passed the seek target), hidden tab/background
  (PiP keeps clock + audio rules), engine stall, two consecutive ticks, picture/audio escalation +3 s. Safari native
  HLS without counters: no picture rule (K22 caught) — but see R2. Firefox has no audio counter → no `audio-silent`.
- Races: a verdict cannot enter the ladder while a step is pending or running (`wantsPlayback` needs no
  `runner.current`; K31 survives because `pictured` is false during a reload anyway). Engine errors during a user
  switch are ignored (`phase === 'switching'`). Clock-frozen hands over to the stall timeline (spinner/hint/ladder),
  so a frozen clock shows nothing for the first ~5 s (4 s rule + second tick) — by design.
- Cost per tick: one `getVideoPlaybackQuality`, one `drawImage`+`getImageData` 16×9 (R11), a ≤ 11-entry drop window,
  a snapshot read; `changed()` only on finding changes. Native targets pay nothing (R6).
- Budget check per verdict: picture-black/frozen → hint after 2 ticks, ladder +3 s → R → S (≤ 3) → card; audio-silent
  same (R8); slideshow 30–60 % → hint with Lower quality (indefinitely, explained), > 60 % → Q, Q, S…; clock-frozen →
  spinner ~6 s, hint ~9 s, T5 at ~20 s. Every path ends compensated or on a specific card — except the false
  positives above (B1–B3, R2–R4), which compensate a healthy playback.

### RecoveryRunner review notes
- Per-step budget 90 s (aborts the step's requests; R never needs it) + start budget 20/30 s for the attached source;
  incident ends after 12 steps (K11 caught) or 3 step-downs (K12 caught); absorption of repeats while a step waits
  (K13 caught); user switches cancel (K14 caught), seeks do not (B4); offline waits forever until 120 s then the card
  (K39 caught), online runs the waiting step (K33) and auto-retries a T1 card (K32); server restart → N → retries stay N
  (K25/K26 caught), V does not (B5); no-op steps end on the card (K40 caught). Nothing loops unbounded within an
  incident; across incidents a recurring failure repeats forever (R5).
- Progress across recoveries: N reports `start` for the new playbackId, the start floor keeps reports from dropping;
  no issue found.

**32 of 40 caught, 8 survived** (K07, K08, K10, K15, K28, K31, K35, K36). The S4b fixes themselves are well guarded (K13/K14/K16/K20/K25/K26/K27/K32/K33/K39/K40 caught); the survivors sit in the step budget, its classification, chained-step tracks, the background/offline start clock and the luma/slideshow thresholds.

### Summary
S4b fixed every bug and risk of the S1–S4 review that its own rows test (P1–P14 now pass or end on a specific card
in bounded time), and the extraction into `RecoveryRunner`, `statusOf` and `HealthMonitor` made the ladder readable
and the S4b mutations mostly lethal. The new work has two kinds of defects. **Regressions of the fixes:** step V
does what N does (stops the old playback) without N's guards, so a single transient failure reloads a stopped
playback and loses the other version (B5); the D09 HEAD probe reaches Safari native HLS, whose routes answer 405, so a
format error ends on "The app hit an unexpected problem" (B6); seeks are not covered by the "user action cancels
the step" fix (B4). **Watchdog false positives that damage healthy playback** — the very thing the requirement
forbids from the other side: pure-black content after a seek (B1), casting (B2), the last seconds of a film (B3),
plus per-element counter proof (R2), audio-only (R3) and low fps (R4). Fix B1–B6 first, then R1/R2/R6, then turn the
8 surviving mutations and the appendix probes into rows.

### Appendix A — previous review probes (re-run verbatim)
The S1–S4 review appendix (journal F10.md "Appendix — probe tests") was extracted unchanged into
`client/src/player/__tests__/zz-review-prev.test.ts`: 10 passed, P6 failed only because its second scripted 404 now
lands on the new playback p2 (see P6b). Logged: P1 `failed start_timeout`, P2 `failed start_timeout 3`, P3 `switches []`,
P4 `currentAudio 2`, P5 `failed end_of_stream`, P8 `[undefined, 100]`, P9 offline hint then card, P10 `4`, P13 hint
cleared, P14 `reloading` with time.

### Appendix B — probe tests (deleted from the worktree; put in `client/src/player/__tests__/` to reproduce)
Expected on d33f57c: P6b, P9b, P1t pass (old findings fixed); N1–N11 and X1 fail (= the findings); X2 passes and logs
290 s (R13). N7–N9 drive the real `WebEngine` with `FakeVideoElement`; N10 the pure `Watchdog`.

```ts
import type { EngineEvent } from '@/player/engines';
import { WebEngine } from '@/player/engines/web-engine.web';
import { Watchdog } from '@/player/health/watchdog';
import { classify } from '@/player/recovery/classify';
import { harness, newController, reply } from '@/../jest/player/harness';
import { FakeVideoElement } from '@/../jest/player/library-fakes';
import { fakeNetwork, playFor, playing, settle, starts, TICKS } from '@/../jest/player/play';
import type { PlaybackController } from '@/player/controller';

jest.mock('@/player/engines', () => jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness').enginesModule());
jest.mock('hls.js', () => jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/library-fakes').hlsJsModule());

beforeEach(() => {
  harness.reset();
  jest.useFakeTimers();
});
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

const exo = (status: number) => `Source error: InvalidResponseCodeException: Response code: ${status}`;
const decode = 'MediaCodecVideoRenderer error: decoder init failed';
const hints = (c: PlaybackController, keys: string[]) => () => {
  const key = c.status.hint?.key;
  if (key && keys.at(-1) !== key) keys.push(key);
};

describe('previous review probes, adapted to S4b', () => {
  it('P6b server restart + one 503 on the new start: the retry is a new start, dead p1 never reloaded', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
    const p1 = c.playback!.playbackId!;
    harness.server.answer('start', reply.error(503, 'server_starting'));
    harness.engine.fail(exo(404));
    await settle();
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
    const uris = harness.engine.sources.map((s) => s.uri);
    console.log('P6b', uris, c.phase, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried?.map((a) => a.step + a.category)));
    expect(uris.filter((u) => u.includes(`/${p1}`))).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(c.phase).toBe('playing');
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('P9b offline at start: back online the start continues on its own', async () => {
    const network = fakeNetwork();
    harness.server.answer('start', reply.offline());
    const c = newController({ network });
    network.set(false);
    await c.start();
    const offlineHint = c.status.hint?.key;
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000);
    network.set(true);
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(3_000);
    console.log('P9b', offlineHint, c.phase, starts().length);
    expect(offlineHint).toBe('offline');
    expect(c.phase).toBe('playing');
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('P1t a reload that never shows a picture ends on a card in bounded time', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
    harness.engine.fail(decode);
    await settle();
    let seconds = 0;
    const keys: string[] = [];
    while (c.phase !== 'failed' && seconds < 1200) {
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
      hints(c, keys)();
      seconds += 1;
    }
    console.log('P1t', seconds, c.failure?.code, keys, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried?.map((a) => `${a.step}${a.category}:${a.code}`)));
    expect(seconds).toBeLessThan(180);
  });
});

describe('new probes S5 + S4b', () => {
  it('N1 a pure-black stretch after a seek (frames and sound running) is called "No picture" and reloaded', async () => {
    harness.features.probe = true;
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 0);
    await playFor(20, (s) => ({ position: s, health: { framesPresented: s * 24, audioProgress: s * 1000, luma: 90 } }));
    c.seekTo(1000);
    const keys: string[] = [];
    await playFor(
      35,
      (s) => ({ position: 1000 + s, health: { framesPresented: 480 + s * 24, audioProgress: 20_000 + s * 1000, luma: 0 } }),
      0,
      hints(c, keys)
    );
    console.log('N1', keys, harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length, harness.engine.source?.startPosition);
    expect(harness.engine.load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('N2 casting (Remote Playback connected): the local audio counter stands -> "No sound" + reload', async () => {
    harness.features.probe = true;
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 0);
    await playFor(5, (s) => ({ position: s, health: { framesPresented: s * 24, audioProgress: s * 1000 } }));
    const keys: string[] = [];
    await playFor(15, (s) => ({ position: s, health: { external: true, framesPresented: 120, audioProgress: 5000 } }), 5, hints(c, keys));
    console.log('N2', keys, harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length);
    expect(harness.engine.load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('N3 audio-only source (server says no video, probe cannot tell): "No picture" + reload', async () => {
    harness.features.probe = true;
    const info = { mediaInfo: { durationTicks: 600 * TICKS, audioTracks: [{ index: 1, selected: true, deliveredAs: 'original' }], subtitleTracks: [], video: null } } as never;
    const c = await playing({}, info, 0);
    const keys: string[] = [];
    await playFor(12, (s) => ({ position: s, health: { framesPresented: 0, audioProgress: s * 1000 } }), 0, hints(c, keys));
    console.log('N3', keys, harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length);
    expect(harness.engine.load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('N4 a stall in the last 4 s without an ended event steps the method down instead of finishing', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 0);
    harness.engine.time(596, 600);
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'buffering', buffering: true });
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(16_000);
    await settle();
    console.log('N4', JSON.stringify(harness.server.sent('switch').map((r) => r.body)), c.notice?.kind, c.ended);
    expect(harness.server.sent('switch')).toHaveLength(0);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('N5 a seek while a reload waits is undone: the reload starts where the failure was', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
    harness.engine.fail(exo(503));
    await settle();
    c.seekTo(400);
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
    console.log('N5', harness.engine.sources.map((s) => s.startPosition));
    expect(harness.engine.source?.startPosition).toBe(400);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('N6 other version (V): one 503 on its start reloads the stopped old playback; the other version is lost', async () => {
    const server = harness.server;
    const c = await playing({}, { version: { releaseId: 'r1' } } as never, 0);
    const p1 = c.playback!.playbackId!;
    server.answer('versions', reply.ok({ versions: [{ releaseId: 'r2', rank: 1, predictedMethod: 'direct' }] }));
    harness.engine.time(300, 600);
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'ended' });
    await settle();
    server.answer('start', reply.error(503, 'server_busy'));
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'firstFrame' });
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'ended' });
    await settle();
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
    await settle();
    const uris = harness.engine.sources.map((s) => s.uri);
    console.log('N6', uris, starts().map((s) => s.releaseId), server.sent('stop').map((r) => r.playbackId), c.phase, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried?.map((a) => a.step + a.category)));
    expect(uris.at(-1)).not.toContain(`/${p1}`);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('N11 the same failure every 2.5 min: always a fresh incident, a reload each time, never a step-down', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) {
      harness.engine.fail(decode);
      await settle();
      harness.engine.started();
      harness.engine.time(100 + i * 150 + 1);
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(150_000);
    }
    console.log('N11 loads', harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length, 'switches', harness.server.sent('switch').length);
    expect(harness.server.sent('switch').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    await c.stop();
  });
});

describe('web probe', () => {
  const engines: WebEngine[] = [];
  afterEach(() => engines.splice(0).forEach((e) => e.release()));
  function nativeEngine() {
    const engine = new WebEngine();
    engines.push(engine);
    Object.defineProperty(engine, 'mode', { value: 'native' });
    const video = new FakeVideoElement();
    (engine as unknown as { attach(video: unknown): void }).attach(video);
    const events: EngineEvent[] = [];
    engine.subscribe((event) => events.push(event));
    return { engine, video, events };
  }

  it('N7 Safari: counters proven on a direct-play source are trusted on the next native-HLS source', async () => {
    const { engine, video } = nativeEngine();
    engine.load({ uri: 'http://server.test/api/v1/stream/p1', kind: 'progressive' });
    video.present(10);
    await engine.readHealth();
    video.present(10);
    await engine.readHealth();
    engine.load({ uri: 'http://server.test/api/v1/transcode/t/master.m3u8', kind: 'hls' });
    (video as unknown as { presentedFrames: number }).presentedFrames = 0;
    const health = await engine.readHealth();
    console.log('N7', health.framesPresented);
    expect(health.framesPresented).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('N8 Safari native HLS: MediaError 4 probes the playlist with HEAD; 405 turns a decode error into T11 http_405', async () => {
    const fetchMock = jest.fn(async () => ({ status: 405 }));
    globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof fetch;
    const { engine, video, events } = nativeEngine();
    engine.load({ uri: 'http://server.test/api/v1/transcode/t/master.m3u8', kind: 'hls' });
    video.present();
    video.tick(1);
    video.fail(4);
    await Promise.resolve();
    await Promise.resolve();
    const failure = events.find((e) => e.type === 'error') as Extract<EngineEvent, { type: 'error' }>;
    const classified = classify({ kind: 'engine', engine: 'web', ...failure });
    console.log('N8', fetchMock.mock.calls, failure, classified);
    expect(classified.category).toBe('T7');
  });

  it('N9 the HEAD probe has no timeout: a hanging HEAD swallows the media error', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = jest.fn(() => new Promise(() => undefined)) as unknown as typeof fetch;
    const { engine, video, events } = nativeEngine();
    engine.load({ uri: 'http://server.test/api/v1/stream/p1', kind: 'progressive' });
    video.present();
    video.tick(1);
    video.fail(2);
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(120_000);
    console.log('N9', events.filter((e) => e.type === 'error').length, engine.getSnapshot().state);
    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === 'error')).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('watchdog unit', () => {
  it('N10 low-fps content: the first frame interval after a reset is judged at the 3 s default', () => {
    const w = new Watchdog();
    const ctx = { wantsPlayback: true, visible: true, pictureInPicture: false, settling: false, hasVideo: true, hasAudio: undefined };
    const verdicts: string[] = [];
    for (let s = 0; s <= 9; s += 1) {
      const found = w.observe(
        { at: s * 1000, position: s, playing: true, buffering: false, health: { framesPresented: 100 + Math.floor(s / 8) } },
        ctx
      );
      verdicts.push(`${s}:${found.verdict}/${found.level}`);
    }
    console.log('N10', verdicts.join(' '));
    expect(verdicts.filter((v) => !v.includes(':ok'))).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
// zz-review-extra.test.ts
import { harness, reply } from '@/../jest/player/harness';
import { playFor, playing, settle } from '@/../jest/player/play';
import type { PlaybackController } from '@/player/controller';

jest.mock('@/player/engines', () => jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness').enginesModule());

beforeEach(() => {
  harness.reset();
  jest.useFakeTimers();
});
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

const exo = (status: number) => `Source error: InvalidResponseCodeException: Response code: ${status}`;
const hints = (c: PlaybackController, keys: string[]) => () => {
  const key = c.status.hint?.key;
  if (key && keys.at(-1) !== key) keys.push(key);
};

it('X1 the video track ends 8 s before the audio: "picture stuck" and a reload in the last seconds', async () => {
  harness.features.probe = true;
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 0);
  const keys: string[] = [];
  await playFor(12, (s) => ({ position: 580 + s, health: { framesPresented: s * 24, audioProgress: s * 1000 } }));
  await playFor(8, (s) => ({ position: 580 + s, health: { framesPresented: 12 * 24, audioProgress: s * 1000 } }), 12, hints(c, keys));
  console.log('X1', keys, harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length, harness.engine.source?.startPosition);
  expect(harness.engine.load).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  await c.stop();
});

it('X2 a new start that never gets ready: how long until the card', async () => {
  const c = await playing({}, {}, 100);
  const starting = () => reply.ok(harness.server.playback({ playbackId: 'slow', state: 'starting', pollAfterMs: 1000 } as never));
  harness.server.answer('start', starting(), starting(), starting());
  harness.server.answer('poll', ...Array.from({ length: 1000 }, starting));
  harness.engine.fail(exo(404));
  await settle();
  const keys: string[] = [];
  let seconds = 0;
  while (c.phase !== 'failed' && seconds < 900) {
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
    hints(c, keys)();
    seconds += 1;
  }
  console.log('X2', seconds, c.failure?.code, keys, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried?.map((a) => `${a.step}${a.category}:${a.code}`)));
  expect(seconds).toBeLessThan(400);
});
```

## Slice S4d — fixes for the code review S5 + S4b (code only, no devices)

Worked 2026-10-05 03:50–(see verification) in `../streamarr-f10` on top of the uncommitted S4c + S8 state; nothing
committed. The reviewer's appendix probes were first run unchanged on this state (12 of 16 failed: N1–N11, X1; P6b,
P9b, P1t, X2 passed), then every finding was fixed at the root and each probe became a permanent row (the probe files
were deleted afterwards). Proof that each row fails without its fix: one reverting mutation per fix (D01–D24 below).
No S9a web-audit FAIL rows had arrived by the end of the slice.

**Bugs**

| # | Root cause | Fix | Row |
|---|---|---|---|
| B1 luma after a seek | the black rule lived on the watchdog *baseline* (reset by every seek/pause) and ignored moving counters | the watchdog keeps a per-**source** record (`newSource()` from `attach`, untouched by seeks/guards): pure black must start within 1 s of the load, stay inside the first 60 s of clock after the load, last 20 s, and **neither frames nor sound may move** (moving = black content: overtures, black openings). The web probe now samples the picture only while frames stand and only in that first minute (R11 cost) | D33 "a pure-black stretch reached by a seek …"; watchdog unit "black reached by a seek is content", "black with frames or sound moving …", K08 window, "a new source starts the minute over"; web-engine-health "samples the picture only while frames stand …" |
| B2 casting | `external` turned off only the picture rules | `local = external !== true` gates picture, audio and slideshow: while casting only the clock rules run (design § 2 a) | A20 "casting …"; watchdog unit "casting (external playback) …" |
| B3 end margin | the C13 margin lived in `onEngineError` only | `nearEnd()` (pictured, position ≥ duration − 12 s): a stall there ends the title after 4 s (no Q/S), a frozen clock or a picture/sound verdict there ends it at once (monitor `stall`/`escalate`) | C13 "a stall in the last seconds …"; D34 "the video track ending a few seconds before the audio …" |
| B4 seek during a pending step | the runner kept the position captured at the failure | `seekTo` → `runner.reposition(target)` for a waiting step; audio/subtitle picks → `runner.retrack(tracks)` | C14 "a seek while a reload waits …" (hint shows 6:40, reload at 400) |
| B5 V without N's guards | only `N` set `sourceDead`/`replacing` | `N` and `V` both mark the old playback dead and ignore its errors; the chosen release is kept in the `Recovery` (`runner.choose`), so a retry after V's failed start starts that release | B06 "other version: one 503 …" (starts `[_, r2, r2]`, p1 never reloaded) |
| B6 HEAD 405 on Safari HLS | the D09 probe ran for every non-hls.js source; transcode routes are GET-only | the probe runs for `progressive` (direct play, whose route has HEAD) only; and at the root `classify` only uses **telling** statuses (401/403 → T2 capability gone, 404/410, 416, ≥ 500); 405 or any other 4xx is ignored and the reason decides (never `http_405`) | D09 "Safari native HLS is never probed …", "a direct-play HEAD answered 405 adds no status …"; classify unit "a media request answered with a status that tells nothing (405) …" |

**Risks**

| # | Fix | Row |
|---|---|---|
| R1 HEAD without timeout | `PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS` 5 s via `Promise.race` (fetch may ignore the abort) → status 0, the error goes on | D09 "a HEAD that never answers gives up after 5 s …" |
| R2 counter proof per element | `framesProven`, `audioProven`, `audioFirst` reset on every load; the frame counter is reported per source (`framesBase` at load) | web-engine-health "counter proof belongs to the source …" |
| R3 audio-only | `hasVideo` is `false` when the server says `video: null` | D33 "an audio-only file …" |
| R4 low fps after a seek | the watchdog keeps the source's frame interval across resets (measured between whole frame moves, max of the measurements); frames already shown but no interval known → 10 s before "frozen" | watchdog unit "low-fps content: the frame interval of the source survives a seek", "frames shown before the first look …" (N10) |
| R5 recurring failure | the runner remembers which code started each incident; the third incident of the same code within 15 min starts at **S** for T5/T6/T7 (not for network/session failures) | D12 "the same decoder failure every 2.5 minutes …" |
| R6 no watchdog on native | the monitor ticks on every engine; without a probe only the clock rule can fire (cost: one snapshot read per second). Comment fixed. Four existing rows that let a "playing" engine stand still for a minute now use `playOn()` (a running clock) — that was a frozen clock the new rule rightly catches | D35 "an engine without a probe …" |
| R7 hls.js retries vs stall ladder | hls.js reports its own retries (`loadRetry` event with the status); a stall within 20 s of a 5xx retry (not 504) enters the ladder as T6 (reload after the backoff), not T5 (lower quality) | C14 "a stall while hls.js retries a 503 …" |
| R8 design A step | implemented in S4c (A after the reload for `audio_silent`, before for `audio_decode_error`); journaled there | (S4c rows) |
| R9 phase leak | `wait()` restores the phase before a step's failed switch enters the ladder (never clobbers a step that already started) | E18 "a step whose switch ends in a failed playback …" |
| R10 frozen a11y label | the label is the live text; the announcement stays one per hint key | E11 "the screen reader label follows the live hint …" |
| R11 luma cost | see B1 | — |
| R12 E16 generic card | device caps failures keep `device_caps_unavailable`; anything else thrown before the start (controller construction) is `player_internal_error`. Not rendered in jest (play-screen) — S9 check | — |
| R13 step budget / 290 s | `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 60 s without server progress (each new server state of a new start re-arms it, absolute `STEP_MAX_MS` 5 min); and per incident the spinner stays at most **60 s** (`INCIDENT_STUCK_MS`, time since the viewer lost the picture, paused while the server reports progress) before the card — except categories with their own budget and hint (offline T1, T3, T4 countdown, T9 stream wait, T10). The server state is not shown in the running hint yet (open) | B25 "a new start that never gets ready ends on the card after a minute …" (card `step_timeout` at 60 s), "a new start whose server keeps reporting progress gets the time it needs" |

## Live checks for S9 — S4d (Dev World 39300 with B12 + B13; one device at a time)
- **B1 (Chrome, hls.js):** a remux title with a long black opening or a black stretch mid-film (or the Dev World test
  pattern with `luma` black): seek into the black → no "No picture", no reload, no step-down; sound runs. The DevTools
  performance panel shows no `getImageData` once frames move or after the first minute.
- **B2 (Chrome Cast / Safari AirPlay):** start casting from the global media controls (Remote Playback) → the local tab
  shows no "No sound"/"No picture", no reload; stop casting → playback continues locally.
- **B3:** `seg_stall {target: video, seconds: 60}` armed for the last segment (or `throttle` near the end) → the end
  card / up-next appears within ~4 s of the stall, no "Lowering the quality", no `/switch` in the server log.
- **B4:** `seg_status {status: 503, retryAfter: 10, mode: once}` → "Problem on the server. Retrying in 10 s…" →
  scrub to +5 min → the hint's time follows, the reload starts at the scrubbed position.
- **B5:** a title with two releases; `direct_truncate {percent: 50}` on r1 and `api_status {target: api:start,
  status: 503, mode: once}` armed for the version switch → "Trying another version…", "busy, retrying", then r2 plays
  at the position; the server log shows no reload of the stopped r1 playback.
- **B6 (Safari macOS / iPhone simulator Safari, native HLS):** a transcode whose codec Safari cannot play (or
  `seg_corrupt {mode: garbage}` on init) → MediaError 4 → "Trying another way to play", never "The app hit an
  unexpected problem — http_405"; the network panel shows no HEAD to `master.m3u8`. `curl -I …/master.m3u8` answers 405
  (confirms the route).
- **R1:** a direct-play title, then block the server with a firewall rule that drops packets (no RST) → the media error
  surfaces within ~5 s ("Connection lost") instead of a quality step.
- **R5:** `seg_corrupt {mode: mdat}` re-armed every 2.5 min → third time: "Trying another way to play" with the
  step-down notice, not a fourth reload.
- **R6 (Google TV AVD, iPhone simulator; expo-video):** `seg_stall {target: video}` mid-play with no buffering event
  from the engine → spinner after ~8 s, "Loading takes longer than usual" after ~11 s, the T5 ladder after ~23 s.
- **R7 (Chrome):** `seg_status {status: 503, retryAfter: 10, mode: {count: 3}}` → "Problem on the server. Retrying…",
  a reload; never "Lowering the quality" for it.
- **R12 (any):** force a throw in the controller constructor (dev build) → card "The player hit an internal error",
  code `player_internal_error`, never "Something went wrong".
- **R13:** `start_hang {seconds: 600}` + `playback_gone` mid-play → "The server ended this playback. Restarting at …"
  for at most ~60 s, then the card "The server took too long" (`step_timeout`); with `transcode_slow` reporting states
  the restart is allowed to take longer.
- **R10 (TalkBack / VoiceOver):** the busy countdown is announced once; focusing the hint later reads the current
  seconds.

#### F10 S9a — web engines under Dev World B12 faults (live audit)

Auditor: unattended subagent · 2026-10-05 03:26–04:46 CEST · worktree `/Users/til/Development/streamarr-f10w` @ d33f57c (read-only, no product change)
Setup: Metro web from f10w on :8083 (`EXPO_PUBLIC_TEST_MUTED=1`), headless Chrome for Testing 149 (`--mute-audio --disable-gpu`, fresh profile `/tmp/ri/s9a/profile`, CDP :9223, 1280×800) driven by a CDP daemon (`/tmp/ri/s9a/cdpd.mjs`, status sampler `/tmp/ri/s9a/st.js`); iPhone 18 Pro simulator Safari (iOS 27) for D09/D10. Dev World 39300, faults scoped to my playbacks (`playbackId` / `next:anna`; one `workId` fault for B06, cleared right after). Fault ids f1–f54 were all mine; all cleared, `GET /devworld/faults` = `[]` at the end.
Screenshots: `streamarr-f10w/docs/client/screenshots/F10/s9a/` (JPEG). Status values = `__streamarrPlayer.status/health/runner.current/failure`, sampled every 0.25–3 s.
Dev World titles are 60–180 s clips, and hls.js buffers a whole remux clip in ~1 s. Mid-play faults therefore need a short buffer: a `throttle {video, 1100 kbps}` (`next:anna`) keeps the buffer small, and the throttle is removed right after the row fault is armed.

Counts: PASS 17 · FAIL 12 · NOT REPRODUCIBLE 13.

### FAIL rows — repro and suspected code

1. **START / C21 / C12 / C08 / D29 / D36 — black 0:00 with neither spinner nor hint (root cause shared).**
   - Repro: arm `{"fault":"seg_delay","scope":{"next":"anna"},"target":"video","params":{"ms":6000},"mode":{"count":2}}` and open `/play/new?workId=tmdb-movie-45745&start=90` in Chrome. You get 6.5 s of black 0:00 with no spinner.
   - Mechanism: in hls.js mode the web engine emits a `time` event with `currentTime 0` before hls.js applies its `startPosition`. The `startSeek` guard in `client/src/player/engines/web-engine.web.tsx:223-236` only applies in native mode, where `startSeek.target` is 0. The controller treats any clock ≥ 0.5 s away from `loadPosition` as a picture, at `client/src/player/controller.ts:700` (`if (!this.pictured && Math.abs(event.position - this.loadPosition) >= 0.5) this.onPicture()`).
   - Result: `onPicture()` (`controller.ts:918-924`) sets `pictured=true` and `loadingSince=0`. That removes the loading spinner (`recovery/status.ts:89-94`) and the start budget (`controller.ts:1059-1061`), so a source that never delivers stays black for good. This is the C12 after step-down, D36 and C21 cases.
   - Every reload, new start or step-down at position > 0 (`controller.ts:744-748`) hits the same path.
   - Fix direction: count a picture only on the engine's `firstFrame`, or on a clock that moved from the load position.
2. **C10 503 (and the interim hint of C10-404, C17, P4) — a server error is shown as "buffering" with "Lower quality".**
   - hls.js retries fragment errors 6× with up to 8 s between them (`FRAG_LOAD_POLICY.errorRetry`, `web-engine.web.tsx:38-45`), and non-fatal errors are dropped (`web-engine.web.tsx:369`). The app therefore learns about a 503/404 only after ~31 s.
   - Meanwhile `statusOf` shows `buffering` + `lowerQuality` (`recovery/status.ts:95-99`, `stallHint`).
   - Fix direction: forward non-fatal `fragLoadError` with an HTTP status so the hint can name the server, or lower `errorRetry` for 5xx/404.
3. **C08 — a slow transcode is called "Slow connection 0.6 of 2.2 Mbit/s".** `stallHint` (`recovery/status.ts`, after line 125) compares the hls.js bandwidth estimate with the bitrate. With `method === 'transcode'` the measured throughput is the server's encode rate, so the server-slow hint ("The server converts slower than playback", C08) never shows.
4. **D36 / C20 — a failed audio split goes down the quality/network ladder.**
   - Repro: `{"fault":"split_abort","scope":{"playbackId":P},"rendition":"1","params":{"afterBytes":4096},"mode":"always"}` mid-play (Sintel, German audio).
   - No `audioError` is emitted: `audioErrorCode` in `client/src/player/engines/hls-audio-error.ts` does not match aborted audio fragment loads. So it becomes a T5 stall → Q → black (item 1) → "Verbindung unterbrochen" loop with no card after 3+ min.
   - Expected: "Kein Ton. Audio wird neu geladen …" → `/switch {audioFallback}`.
5. **P2/D41, P8/C14, D10 — an unbounded switch.**
   - After `/switch` (to transcode) the server keeps the playback in `starting` (85 s in P2, 3+ min in P8 and D10) while the client polls every 0.5 s.
   - `statusOf` returns `NONE` for `phase === 'switching'`, and `STATE_BUDGET_MS.starting` (45 s) is only checked for `phase === 'starting'` (`recovery/status.ts:78-85`). The viewer sees "Wird umgeschaltet… / Stream wird geöffnet." over a frozen frame with no budget, hint or card.
   - Server side to check: why a transcode run started by `/switch` on a playback with an active `seg_status`/`seg_corrupt`/`playlist_endless` fault never reaches `ready`. Possibly the Dev World fault also catches the server's own readiness read, so this may be a harness artefact. The missing client budget is real either way.
6. **D10 (Safari native HLS).**
   - A media playlist without `ENDLIST` makes Safari treat the stream as live. After the reload or step-down, playback restarted at 0:21 instead of 0:59 (duration "unbestimmt"). The ladder then continued from the wrong position ("es geht weiter bei 0:29").
   - The status hint's action button renders as an **empty white pill with no label** on iPhone Safari (`d10-safari-hint-blank-button.jpg`; Chrome renders "Niedrigere Qualität"). Suspect: the status-layer button text style in `client/src/screens/player/player-status.tsx` on WebKit.
7. **E09.**
   - After `engine.load` throws in an N step, the old hls.js source keeps playing until its buffer runs out. The runner then classifies the stall as T5 and runs Q.
   - Q calls `/switch` on a playback the server already closed → `playback_not_found` → the card "Wiedergabe beendet" instead of "Interner Fehler im Player / player_internal_error".
   - Suspect: the N/restore path in `controller.ts` (no engine teardown when `load` throws; the failure is not terminal for the incident) and runner categorisation.
8. **B06.**
   - With `transcode_never_start` on every HLS start, the ladder ran N, N, N and then a card, although a direct-play mp4 release of the same title exists. The V step ("Trying another version at …") never ran.
   - Suspect: `client/src/player/recovery/ladder.ts` (V not reachable from category of `transcode_failed` at N) or `recovery/other-version.ts` (`nextVersion`).
9. **P8 minor:** `retryAfter: 10` showed as "Neuer Versuch in 5 s". Either `Retry-After` is not readable cross-origin by hls.js/fetch, or it is not forwarded.

## Slice S4d — S9a fixes (live web audit, folded into S4d)

Worked 2026-10-05 04:47–(see verification) on top of the S4d code above; nothing committed. Every FAIL row of the
audit got a row test (C, D, E matrix files, "S9a" in the title) and a revert mutation (A1–A20 below) that makes it fail.
The audit ran d33f57c (before S4c/S4d); rows that S4c/S4d had already changed are noted.

| # | S9a row(s) | Root cause | Fix | Row test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | START, C21, C12/C18, C08, D29, D36 (black 0:00, no spinner, no hint) | hls.js reports `time 0` before it applies `startPosition`; the controller counted any clock ≥ 0.5 s away from the load position as a picture, so `pictured=true` removed the loading spinner and the start budget — a source that never delivers stayed black, and every reload/new start/step-down at a position > 0 hit it | a picture is the engine's `firstFrame`, or a clock that **runs**: two readings of this source in forward order at most 3 s apart (`clockRuns`); a single jump (0 → 90) is never a picture | E03 "hls.js reports 0:00 before it applies the start position …" (spinner, `startSlow` at 4 s, reload at the start budget, at 90); E03 "a clock that runs on from the start position counts as a picture" |
| 2 | C10 503/404, C17, P4 interim ("buffering" + Lower quality ~31 s) | hls.js retried fragments 6× (S4c had cut this to 3, Retry-After-aware); non-fatal errors reached the controller only after 15 s; a single-level stream is retried by hls.js even on a 4xx (its `noAlternate` branch ignores `shouldRetry`) | non-fatal network errors are forwarded at once as `loadRetry {status}`; while stalled with a recent 4xx/5xx retry the status says "The server had a problem with this part of the video. Retrying…" at once, with **no** actions; the retry policy's `maxNumRetry` is 0 after a 401/403/404/410 answer (the ladder starts anew at once), 3 for 5xx | C14 "while hls.js retries a 503 the stall says 'problem on the server' at once …"; engine units "a non-fatal 503 is forwarded …", "a 404/410 answer leaves the retry to the ladder …", "the wrapped loader notes the status …" |
| 3 | C08 (slow transcode called "Slow connection 0.6 of 2.2 Mbit/s") | the hls.js bandwidth estimate includes the time the server needs before the first byte | the web probe keeps the last video segment's **wait before the first byte** and **transfer after it** (`EngineHealth.fetch`, from `frag.stats.loading`); for remux/transcode a segment that waited longer than it took to arrive is "The server converts slower than playback"; "Slow connection x of y" is measured from the transfer alone | C08 "a transcode whose segments wait for the server …"; C03 "a remux segment that arrives slowly after a quick first byte is the network …" (keeps the C03 PASS); engine unit "reads the last video segment …" |
| 4 | D36/C20 (aborted audio split → quality/network loop, no card after 3 min) | an aborted audio fragment answers status 0, `audioErrorCode` only knows 404/500, so it became a network error | the engine calls an audio fragment/playlist failure while video fragments still arrive (last 15 s) an **audio rendition** failure: non-fatal → `loadRetry {audio: true}` (status: "No sound. Reloading the audio…" at once), fatal → `audioRendition:<details>` → `audio_rendition_failed` (T7); ladder: R (reload, hint "No sound …") → A (`/switch {audioFallback}`) → card ("The sound couldn't be loaded", Retry / Other version) — never Q, S or the network ladder | C20 "an aborted audio split: 'No sound', reload of the audio, the server converts the audio, then the card …"; engine units "an audio fragment failing while video still loads …", "without video arriving it stays a network failure" |
| 5 | P2/D41, P8/C14, D10 (switch stuck in `starting` 85 s – 3+ min, no budget) | `statusOf` returned nothing for phase `switching`; a viewer's switch (and its restore) had no budget at all (steps had the S4d step budget) | a viewer's switch gets the step budget: a minute without a new server state, five at most (`beginSwitch`/`extendSwitch`); past it the old playback counts as switched away and the ladder starts anew there (`step_timeout`, detached) and ends on the card by its caps; the switching card shows "The server is still preparing the video" after the state budget (45 s for `starting`) | E18 "a viewer switch stuck in 'starting': the switching card explains after 45 s and the ladder takes over after 60 s" |
| 6 | D10 Safari native HLS (position lost; empty white button) | Safari calls a playlist without ENDLIST live: duration `Infinity`, the reload starts in its live window (0:21 for 0:59), and a picture verdict escalated with the watchdog's last-moving position (0:29) | duration falls back to the server's when the engine's is not finite; a pictured engine running ≥ 3 s below a start position never reached, with a non-finite duration, is sent back to the position once; picture verdicts below an unreached start position resume at the last good position. The hint action is now a plain pill (`status-action.tsx`: no glass backdrop, no lift transform, no one-line clamp) — the GlassButton combination painted an empty pill on WebKit; this is a fix by reasoning, live check pending | D10 "a reload that Safari starts in its live window …"; `player-status.test` "the label is plain text in its own colour, never clamped …" |
| 7 | E09 (load throws in N → old source plays on → Q → "Playback ended / playback_not_found") | the throw left the old source playing; T11 then reloaded and started anew, the old buffer ran out, the stall became T5 → Q on a playback the server had closed | a throwing `engine.load` pauses and shuts the engine down and rethrows; `player_internal_error` is terminal (card at once, Retry) | E09 "an engine whose load throws stops the old source and ends on the internal-error card …" |
| 8 | B06 (`transcode_never_start` on every start → N, N, N, card; V never ran) | V was only reachable from `no_more_methods` (T7) and T8 | for conversion failures (`transcode_failed`, `ffmpeg_unavailable`, `rendition_split_failed`, `segment_timeout`, `step_timeout`) a detached failure gets one fresh start, then **V** (the best other version at the position), then the card | B06 "transcode_failed on every new start: one more start, then another version at the position …" |
| 9 | P8 ("Retrying in 5 s" for Retry-After 10) | the audited state did not forward Retry-After (S4c does); where CORS hides the header (Expose-Headers is the backend's part) nothing was readable | `retryAfterMs` also reads the error envelope's `retryAfterSeconds` from the XHR body (text or fragment ArrayBuffer) | C14 "a fatal 503 that asks for 10 s counts down from 10 …"; unit "reads retryAfterSeconds from the error envelope …" |

### B13b adoption (folded into the S9a fixes, 2026-10-05 05:28–05:40)
Server follow-up B13b (docs/client/journal/B13.md "## B13b", not yet on 39300):

| Item | Client change | Row test |
|---|---|---|
| 1 new server code `start_timeout` (a start/switch revision bounded at 60 s, actions retry + lower quality) | The client's own code with that name (no picture after `ready`, S3) is renamed **`picture_timeout`** (T7, text and step-down reason unchanged). `start_timeout` is now the server's: **T5** (too slow — the server's own remedy is a lower quality), de/en text "The server took too long to start". Ladder: a failed playback with `start_timeout` (start, new start or a recovery switch — the server does not switch a failed playback) → one fresh start at the position → another version → the card with the server's actions (retry, lowerQuality). A **viewer's** switch that ends in `start_timeout` while the old source still plays (engine never told to load another) keeps that source: `this.playback` back to the previous revision, the previous preferences, phase `playing`, notice "Couldn't switch. The server took too long …" — no new start, no reload. The client-side switching budget stays (defence in depth). | B19 "a viewer switch the server gave up on keeps the old source playing …"; B16 "a start the server gave up on …", "a recovery switch the server gave up on …" |
| 2 Retry-After | hls.js never hands fragment/playlist headers to the app, so the engine no longer reads or forwards any Retry-After (the S9a body/header parsing and `EngineEvent.error.retryAfter` are removed; `hls-retry-after.ts` → `hls-retry.ts` with `statusLoader` + `fragLoadPolicy`). hls.js retries a fragment 3× with a **fixed** 1 / 2 / 4 s backoff (0× after 401/403/404/410), then the ladder waits its own T6 5 s / 15 s and its countdown shows exactly that. Retry-After is read only on the app's own requests (playback API, `/switch`, progress), as before. | C14 "an hls.js fragment 503 …: the fixed 5 s, shown as 5 s"; C14 "a request the app sends itself … Retry-After 10: shown and waited as 10 s"; D01 "a fatal 503 carries its status, never a guessed Retry-After"; hls-retry unit tests |
| 3 hot loop on one corrupt fragment (12,576 fetches of segment 6 in a minute on 39300) | `fragmentFails`: parse/decode errors of the same fragment (`type:level:sn`, main or audio; network errors stay with the policy) are counted per source. Non-fatal: hls.js loading is stopped and restarted after 1 s, then 2 s; the **third** failure stops loading and hands `mediaError:fragParsingError` to the ladder (T7: reload, then another way to play). | D02 "a corrupt fragment that hls.js would fetch again at once: at most three attempts with backoff, then the ladder" — a fake hls.js re-fetches the failing segment every 1 ms while loading: exactly 3 fetches in 60 s, ~1 s and ~2 s apart, loading stopped, one error event |

**Mutations (each alone, restored byte-exact):** B1 keep-old-source off, B2 start_timeout ladder off, B3 category T6,
B4 fragment budget off, B5 budget 3 → 99, B6 no backoff, B7 backoff 1 → 7 s, B8 engine adds a Retry-After: **8/8
caught**. Moved patterns re-run: M32 (`picture_timeout` reason), S17 (`fragLoadPolicy` line), A6 (gone statuses) caught.
Obsolete by design (they mutated the Retry-After-from-hls.js path that no longer exists): S4c R1–R4, S9a A18; B7/B8
replace them.

switch. The server took too long …" while the old picture plays on (no reload); `start_hang {seconds 90}` on a start →

Lower quality. `seg_corrupt {mode garbage, always}` on one segment → server log shows at most 3 hits for that segment

### 2.–5. Findings

Severity:
- **bug** — a probe confirmed a wrong state for the viewer.
- **risk** — plausible, or depends on the platform.
- **test-gap** — a mutation survived or a row is missing.
- **quality** — project rules.

Probe ids refer to the appendix. Line numbers are at 225fe02.

| # | Severity | file:line | What is wrong | Failure scenario (matrix row → what the viewer sees) | Suggested fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | **bug** (Q1) | `player/recovery/status.ts:62`, `player/controller.ts:1310-1336` (statusTick `ready`/`calm`), `recovery/runner.ts:103-104` | During the quiet `playbackAlive` restart (B25/A09), two things go wrong. First, `statusOf` returns NONE unless the *new* source is loading. Second, `statusTick` keeps running the stall timeline and the seek-stall rule while the quiet N step is **running**. When the old source's buffer runs out before the new start is ready, the stall gets no spinner and no hint. After 15 s, `runner.handle(T5 playback_stalled)` arrives while N is running: it is not absorbed, `attached` is false (`sourceDead`), and the result is **G**. The progressing new start is cancelled. | A09/B25: the server expired an idle playback (or restarted). The viewer is on a transcode with about 5–10 s of buffer, and the server needs about 20 s for the new run. The picture freezes with **no spinner and no hint for 15 s**, then the card "playback_stalled" (Lower quality / Other version), although the server was at `starting` and would have been ready seconds later. A seek during the quiet step leads to the same card through the D29 rule. Probe Q1: `playing/-/-` at 5 s and 10 s, `failed playback_stalled` at 15 s, tried `[N T2]`. | (a) `statusOf`: for a quiet recovery, when `stallSince` is set, show the spinner and, after `HINT_MS`, the `restarting` hint (the quiet promise only holds while the old picture moves). (b) `statusTick`: no stall or seek-stall ladder while `recovery?.running` (the step has its own budget). The same guard would also close K31. |
| B2 | **bug** (Q5) | `player/engines/web-engine.web.tsx:478-481` (`fragmentFails`), `recovery/classify.ts:96-97` | The B13b corrupt-fragment budget counts **every** non-network hls.js error that carries a main/audio `frag`. In hls.js 1.7.3, `bufferFullError` (QuotaExceeded, never fatal, retried by hls.js after it shrinks the buffer: "with UHD content, we could get loop of quota exceeded"), `bufferAppendError` and `bufferAppendNoProgress` all carry the frag. The third quota error of the same segment calls `stopLoad` and raises a fatal `mediaError:bufferFullError`, which becomes T7 `decode_error`: R, then a step-down. Row D05 tests `bufferFullError` **without** a `frag`, which hls.js never sends, so it stays green. This is a regression against D05 ("hls.js shrinks its buffer itself; no error, no ladder"). | D05 on memory-tight MSE (Safari iPad, Chrome Android, TV browsers, 4K remux): "Reloading at …", then "Trying another way to play", and the step-down notice "**this device could not decode the video**". The video was decodable; the quota pressure would have cleared by itself. Probe Q5: 3 `stopLoad` calls and one `error mediaError:bufferFullError`. | Budget only parse and decode failures (`fragParsingError`, `fragDecryptError`, `bufferAppendError` with a non-quota error). Leave `bufferFullError` and `bufferAppendNoProgress` to hls.js. Give D05 a realistic `frag`. |
| B3 | **bug** (Q4) | `player/recovery/status.ts:146-147` | `fetch.waitMs > fetch.transferMs` → "The server converts slower than playback" for **remux** too, and for any fast transfer. Time to first byte includes the network RTT, TLS and server latency. On a LAN or a fast connection, a 4 MB segment arrives in 10 ms while the first byte takes 30 ms. | C03/C10 neighbourhood: a remux (no conversion at all) stalls because Wi-Fi drops mid-segment, or a seek far ahead waits. The last *completed* fetch was fast, so the hint says "The server converts slower than playback. Buffering…" with Lower quality. That is the wrong cause and the wrong word (remux does not convert). Probe Q4: `serverSlow` for remux, wait 30 ms, transfer 10 ms. | Limit the rule to `method === 'transcode'`. Require an absolute wait (for example ≥ 1 s, or ≥ the segment duration × 0.5) in addition to `wait > transfer`. Use `stallHint`'s network path otherwise. |
| B4 | **bug** (Q8) | `player/controller.ts:1294-1308` | The `start_stuck` rule runs before the offline check and ignores `this.offline`. If the network drops while the server is `starting`, the "offline" hint ("continues once online") shows. At 2 × 45 s after the last state, the card **T6 start_stuck** ("the server got stuck", Retry/Other version) appears. T6 cards do not retry on their own when the network returns; T1 cards do (A25). | A24/E02: the start runs, the phone loses Wi-Fi in a lift, and after about 90 s the card blames the server instead of waiting for the network. Probe Q8: `offline` hint at 15–75 s, then `start_stuck T6`. | Skip the `start_stuck` rule while `this.offline`, and restart `stateSince` when the network returns (as `restartClocks` does for the other clocks). |
| B5 | **bug** (minor; Q6) | `player/controller.ts:686-716` (`onSubtitleError`/`retrySubtitle`) | A retry that worked leaves `subtitleRetry` at `{state: 'retrying', failures: 1}` forever. Any later failure of the same track (30 min later, an unrelated blip) counts as the second failure. The subtitles go off for good with "Choose other subtitles". | C22: German WebVTT fails once at minute 5 and comes back at minute 6. At minute 40 one segment 503s, and the subtitles stay off for the rest of the film, without a retry. Probe Q6: second notice `retry: ''`. | Clear `subtitleRetry` once the retried track delivers (a `tracks`/cue event after `retrying`), or after a healthy window (for example 2 min, like `INCIDENT_RESET_MS`). |
| R1 | risk | `player/controller.ts:1294-1308`, `recovery/budgets.ts` `STATE_BUDGET_MS.queued` | `start_stuck` fires at 2 × budget for `queued` (120 s), `resolving` (120 s) and `fallback`. A full transcoder queue, or a slow indexer search, is legitimate waiting. The server bounds only `starting` (60 s, `start_timeout`). | E02: an evening with 3 transcodes running, and a fourth start sits `queued` for 2.5 min. The card "stuck" + Retry appears, and Retry queues the start again at the back. | Keep `queued` (and `resolving` while the server reports progress) out of the hard card, or extend it while the queue position moves. |
| R2 | risk (Q7) | `recovery/runner.ts:106-113`, `recovery/ladder.ts:116-118` | "Recurring" (the review R5 fix) also applies to **T6 server errors** (`segment_unavailable` 503, `server_error`) and to T5 network stalls. The third incident in 15 min skips the reload and steps the **method** down. | A server with a flaky disk answers a segment 503 every 4 min. On the third incident the direct play becomes remux, then transcode, which puts more load on the struggling server. The notice says the device could not keep up or decode. Probe Q7: 2 `/switch stepDown` for three 503 incidents. | Restrict `recurring` to device-side codes (T7 decode/picture/audio verdicts, `playback_slideshow`). For T5, prefer Q. For T6, keep the reload/N ladder. |
| R3 | risk | `player/controller.ts:793-799` | The sticky audio conversion inside `newStart` calls `switchPlayback` without the step's `signal`, and its `waitForPlayback` does not report progress to the runner. The step budget (started at the step, extended only by the first wait's states) can cut a slow but legitimate conversion start → `step_timeout` → N again. A stopped controller's switch request also runs on. | D13 + B25: a new start after a conversion takes 40 s, then the audio switch needs 30 s → step_timeout → another new start (and another conversion). | Pass `signal` and the same `runner.progress()` state callback to the second wait. |
| R4 | risk | `player/controller.ts:1518` | The restore after a refused viewer switch (catch path, non-T1 errors) calls `restore(...)` without the switch budget's signal. Only the server's 60 s bound limits it. | B18/B19: `/switch` is answered 409/500, and the restore start hangs in `queued`. The switching card stays until the server decides, with no client budget there. | Pass `signal` as the other call site does. |
| R5 | risk (live) | `player/engines/web-poster.ts:5-20`, `web-engine.web.tsx:351` | `lastFrame` draws and encodes a JPEG (half of the source size, for example 1920×1080 for 4K) synchronously on every hls.js load (reload, step, switch), on the main thread right before teardown. | E18 on weak TV browsers or phones: a 50–150 ms jank at every switch, and the poster is the *last decoded* frame, which after a picture-black/frozen verdict is the broken picture. | Cap the canvas (for example 640 px wide). Skip it when the load comes from a picture verdict (`picture_black`/`frozen`). |
| R6 | risk (a11y/web) | `screens/player/status-action.tsx:17-46` | The plain `Pressable` has no focus style. Keyboard users on web get the browser's default outline at best (RN Web often suppresses it). TV is excluded by design. | E11/E12 web keyboard: Tab onto "Lower quality" shows no visible focus. | Add a focus ring (`focusable` with an `onFocus` state style) as the other web buttons have. |
| N1 | note (not a bug) | `controller.ts:1573-1581` | B19 `keepOldSource` relies on the server still serving the previous revision after a failed `/switch`. If the server closes it, the old buffer runs out, then 404 → T2 → N: compensated, with the notice first. | — | Live check (S9 B19) should confirm which one happens. |

### Platform notes
- **TV focus:**
  - `StatusAction` has `hasTVPreferredFocus={false}`, and TV gets no hint actions (`design.isTV ? []`).
  - The switching card's hint is plain text.
  - Notices are pointer-transparent.
  - The new sign-in card (T3) uses the existing card buttons.
  - I found no path that steals focus.
- **Web vs native:**
  - Everything new in the engine (`loadRetry`, `subtitleError`, fetch timing, poster, fragment budget, HEAD probe) is hls.js or `<video>` only.
  - Native engines still have no subtitle/loadRetry events; the native agent's S6/S7 owns that.
  - `disableRemotePlayback` hides the cast button on Chrome/Edge. Safari AirPlay uses `x-webkit-airplay`, which is unaffected; the review B2 guard covers it.
  - The HEAD probe now runs only for `progressive` sources, so Safari native HLS is safe.
- **Safari:**
  - Without hls.js there is no poster: `lastFrame` runs only with `this.hls`, and a `src`-played frame is tainted anyway.
  - The live-window seek-back and the duration fallback are code-only; the S9 live check is pending.
- **i18n:**
  - en/de key sets are identical in `en/de.json`, `player.en/de.json` and `locales/native`.
  - Every new client code has a text (`audio_rendition_failed`, `network_intercepted`, `start_stuck`, `audio_decode_error`, `subtitle_*`, `empty_media`, `picture_timeout`, server `start_timeout`).
  - Every new hint and notice has a text.
  - The ICU select/plural placeholders differ by design.
- **a11y:**
  - The hint label now follows the live text and is announced once per key (S4b R10 fixed).
  - New hints have no actions where nothing can help (serverRetrying, serverRepairing, convertingAudio).
  - Missing web focus style: R6.

### Summary
The S4d fixes and the S9a fixes hold. All 16 second-review probes now pass. Step budgets are bounded at 60 s per stall
without progress, with 5 min at most. The S9a rows are each guarded, and my mutations on that code were caught.

The new work brings five wrong states, all in the new code paths:
- **B1 (most important):** the silent `playbackAlive` restart is not silent when the old buffer runs dry. The viewer gets a frozen picture with no spinner. Then the stall ladder kills the progressing restart with a "playback stalled" card. This happens exactly in the server-restart / idle-expiry case that B13 was built for.
- **B2:** the B13b corrupt-fragment budget swallows hls.js's own quota-exceeded handling. On memory-tight browsers, a decodable 4K remux is reloaded and stepped down with a wrong "could not decode" reason. Row D05 hides this because its fake error lacks a `frag`.
- **B3:** the C08 "server converts slowly" heuristic misfires on remux and fast connections.
- **B4:** the B16 `start_stuck` card ignores offline, so a network drop during a start ends on a server-blaming card that never retries by itself.
- **B5:** one recovered subtitle failure turns the next one, even an hour later, into "off for good".

The two server-fault risks (recurring step-down for T6, the hard card for `queued`) contradict "compensate, don't
degrade". Nine S4d/S4f guards have no row (surviving mutations). The controller is back to about 2,000 lines, with
recovery policy and a second budget implementation inside it.

### Appendix B — probe tests (deleted from the worktree; put in `client/src/player/__tests__/` to reproduce)
Expected results at 225fe02:
- Fail (= the findings): Q1 (B1), Q4 (B3), Q5 (B2), Q6 (B5), Q7 (R2), Q8 (B4).
- Pass: Q2. It shows that a sign-out card during a viewer switch is not overwritten.
- Fail, but not a finding: Q3. Its server script stays at `starting` for 90 s, which the real server ends at 60 s with `start_timeout`.

```ts
import type { EngineEvent } from '@/player/engines';
import { loadHls, WebEngine } from '@/player/engines/web-engine.web';
import { statusOf } from '@/player/recovery/status';
import { harness, reply } from '@/../jest/player/harness';
import { FakeHls, FakeVideoElement } from '@/../jest/player/library-fakes';
import { fakeNetwork, playing, settle, starts } from '@/../jest/player/play';
import type { PlaybackController } from '@/player/controller';

jest.mock('@/player/engines', () => jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness').enginesModule());
jest.mock('hls.js', () => jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/library-fakes').hlsJsModule());

beforeEach(() => {
  harness.reset();
  jest.useFakeTimers();
});
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

const exo = (status: number) => `Source error: InvalidResponseCodeException: Response code: ${status}`;
const log = (c: PlaybackController, out: string[], at: number) => {
  const s = c.status;
  out.push(`${at}s:${c.phase}/${s.spinner ? 'spin' : '-'}/${s.hint?.key ?? '-'}`);
};
const starting = (id: string) =>
  reply.ok(harness.server.playback({ playbackId: id, state: 'starting', pollAfterMs: 1000 } as never));

describe('review S4c-S4f probes', () => {
  it('Q1 quiet playbackAlive restart + slow server: the old buffer runs out, frozen picture without spinner or hint', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, { method: 'transcode' } as never, 100);
    harness.server.answer('progress', reply.ok({ playbackAlive: false }));
    harness.server.answer('start', starting('q2'));
    harness.server.answer('poll', ...Array.from({ length: 200 }, () => starting('q2')));
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10_000);
    // the old source plays 5 s more from its buffer, then stalls
    harness.engine.time(105);
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'buffering', buffering: true });
    const out: string[] = [];
    for (let s = 1; s <= 40; s += 1) {
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
      if (s % 5 === 0) log(c, out, s);
    }
    console.log('Q1', starts().length, out.join(' '), c.failure?.code, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried));
    expect(c.status.spinner || !!c.status.hint).toBe(true);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('Q2 sign-out card while a viewer switch is in flight: the switch attaches afterwards and hides the card', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, { method: 'transcode' } as never, 100);
    harness.server.answer('switch', starting('p1'));
    harness.server.answer('poll', starting('p1'), starting('p1'), starting('p1'));
    const switching = c.serverSwitch({ maxHeight: 720 } as never);
    await settle();
    harness.server.answer('progress', reply.error(401, 'refresh_session_revoked'));
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10_000);
    const failedAt = `${c.phase}/${c.failure?.code}`;
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10_000);
    console.log('Q2', failedAt, '->', c.phase, c.failure?.code, harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length);
    expect(c.phase).toBe('failed');
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('Q3 start_stuck card fires during a recovery N whose own start progresses', async () => {
    // first start: 'starting' for 40 s, then a transient 503 on the poll; the N start then progresses slowly
    harness.server.answer('start', starting('s1'), starting('s2'));
    harness.server.answer(
      'poll',
      ...Array.from({ length: 40 }, () => starting('s1')),
      reply.error(503, 'server_busy'),
      ...Array.from({ length: 200 }, () => starting('s2'))
    );
    const { newController } = jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness');
    const c: PlaybackController = newController({});
    void c.start();
    const out: string[] = [];
    for (let s = 1; s <= 110; s += 1) {
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
      if (s % 10 === 0) log(c, out, s);
      if (c.phase === 'failed') break;
    }
    console.log('Q3', out.join(' '), c.failure?.code, starts().length, JSON.stringify(c.failure?.tried), JSON.stringify(harness.server.requests.map(r=>r.route).filter((r,i,a)=>r!==a[i-1])));
    expect(c.failure?.code).not.toBe('start_stuck');
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('Q4 a remux stall after a quick LAN segment (wait 30 ms > transfer 10 ms) is called "the server converts slowly"', () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const status = statusOf({
      phase: 'playing',
      now,
      loadingSince: 0,
      offline: false,
      paused: false,
      stallSince: now - 6_000,
      seekAt: 0,
      recovery: null,
      health: null,
      serverState: 'ready',
      serverStateSince: 0,
      method: 'remux',
      bitrateKbps: 8000,
      bandwidthBps: 2_000_000,
      fetch: { waitMs: 30, transferMs: 10, bytes: 4_000_000 },
    } as never);
    console.log('Q4', JSON.stringify(status));
    expect(status.hint?.key).not.toBe('serverSlow');
  });

  it('Q5 bufferFullError (hls.js handles it itself) counts against the corrupt-fragment budget and goes to the ladder', async () => {
    const engine = new WebEngine();
    (engine as unknown as { attach(video: unknown): void }).attach(new FakeVideoElement());
    await loadHls();
    const events: EngineEvent[] = [];
    engine.subscribe((event) => void events.push(event));
    engine.load({ uri: 'http://server.test/master.m3u8', kind: 'hls' });
    const hls = FakeHls.last;
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
      hls.error('mediaError', 'bufferFullError', { fatal: false, frag: { type: 'main', sn: 6, level: 0 } });
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5_000);
    }
    console.log('Q5', hls.stopLoad.mock.calls.length, JSON.stringify(events.filter((e) => e.type === 'error')));
    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === 'error')).toBe(false);
    engine.release();
  });

  it('Q6 a subtitle retry that worked is never retried again: a failure 30 min later stays off for good', async () => {
    const subtitleTracks = [{ index: 3, language: 'de', title: 'German', deliveredAs: 'webvtt', selected: true }];
    const c = await playing({}, { method: 'remux', mediaInfo: { durationTicks: 6e10, audioTracks: [], subtitleTracks } } as never, 100);
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: { audio: [], subtitles: [{ id: 's0', label: 'German', language: 'de', selected: true }] } } as never);
    await settle();
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'subtitleError', code: 'subtitle_unavailable' });
    const first = c.notice?.params;
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(61_000);
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'tracks', tracks: { audio: [], subtitles: [{ id: 's0', label: 'German', language: 'de', selected: true }] } } as never);
    for (let i = 0; i < 1800; i += 1) {
      harness.engine.time(161 + i);
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
    }
    harness.engine.emit({ type: 'subtitleError', code: 'subtitle_unavailable' });
    console.log('Q6', JSON.stringify(first), JSON.stringify(c.notice?.params));
    expect(c.notice?.params?.retry).toBe('later');
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('Q7 three separate 503 incidents within 15 min: the third steps the method down (server fault, not the device)', async () => {
    const c = await playing({}, { method: 'direct' } as never, 100);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
      harness.engine.fail(exo(503));
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(6_000);
      harness.engine.started();
      harness.engine.time(110 + i * 200);
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(180_000);
    }
    console.log('Q7 loads', harness.engine.load.mock.calls.length, 'switches', JSON.stringify(harness.server.sent('switch').map((r) => r.body)));
    expect(harness.server.sent('switch')).toHaveLength(0);
    await c.stop();
  });

  it('Q8 network lost while the server is starting: after 90 s the card blames the server (start_stuck), not the network', async () => {
    const network = fakeNetwork();
    harness.server.answer('start', starting('o1'));
    harness.server.answer('poll', ...Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => starting('o1')), ...Array.from({ length: 300 }, () => reply.offline()));
    const { newController } = jest.requireActual('@/../jest/player/harness');
    const c: PlaybackController = newController({ network });
    void c.start();
    await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5_500);
    network.set(false);
    const out: string[] = [];
    for (let s = 1; s <= 120; s += 1) {
      await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
      if (s % 15 === 0) log(c, out, s);
      if (c.phase === 'failed') break;
    }
    console.log('Q8', out.join(' '), c.failure?.code, c.failure?.category);
    expect(c.failure?.code).not.toBe('start_stuck');
    await c.stop();
  });
});

```


## Slice S4g — fixes for the code review S4c-S4f (code only, no devices)

Worked 2026-10-05 06:04–07:05 in `../streamarr-f10` on 225fe02; nothing committed. The reviewer's
appendix probes were first run unchanged on 225fe02: Q1, Q3, Q4, Q5, Q6, Q7, Q8 failed, Q2 passed (as the review says).
After the fixes Q1, Q4, Q5, Q6, Q8 pass. Q3 is the reviewer's own "fails but not a finding" (its script holds `starting`
for 90 s, which the server ends at 60 s). Q7's two step-downs came from the probe's frozen fake clock (the engine says
`playing` for 180 s without a time event → the S4d clock rule → T5 stall ladder), not from recurring 503s; the
permanent row uses a running clock and also proves R2 (0 step-downs for three 503 incidents). The probe file was
deleted; each finding is a permanent row (below). Proof that each row fails without its fix: the G-mutations.

| # | Root cause | Fix | Row test |
|---|---|---|---|
| B1 quiet restart + old buffer runs dry | `statusOf` hid everything for a quiet step unless the new source loaded; `statusTick`'s stall/seek ladders ran while the quiet N step was **running** and turned the stall into G (`sourceDead`) | `status.ts`: a quiet step whose old picture stalls shows the spinner, after 4 s the step's hint ("The server ended this playback. Restarting at …"). `statusTick`: `calm` excludes a running step — the step's own budget decides (also closes the reviewer's K31 note) | B25 "a silent restart whose old buffer runs dry …", "a seek during the silent restart does not cancel it either" |
| B2 bufferFullError counted as corrupt | `fragmentFails` budgeted every non-network error with a `frag`; hls.js 1.7.3 sends `frag` with `bufferFullError`/`bufferAppendNoProgress` | only `fragParsingError`, `fragDecryptError`, `bufferAppendError` count (`CORRUPT_FRAGMENT`) | D05 now sends the realistic error (with `frag`) three times: no error, no `stopLoad`, no recovery |
| B3 "converts slower" for remux / fast LAN | one segment's `wait > transfer` | the web probe keeps the server waits and media length of the last 3 video segments; `conversionRate = media / waited`; `serverSlow` only for `method === 'transcode'` with a rate below 1 (a remux never "converts") | C08 "remux (no conversion) or a quick first byte: never 'the server converts' …" (+ a transcode at rate 0.66 → serverSlow) |
| B4 start_stuck while offline | the rule ignored `offline` and the state clock ran through the outage | `startStuck()` (recovery/stall.ts) is false while offline; `restartClocks` also restarts `stateSince` on every network change | B16 "offline while the server starts …", B16 "a short outage between two polls 80 s into 'starting' …" |
| B5 subtitles off for good after one good retry | the retry state was never forgotten | `SubtitleRetry` (new `player/subtitle-retry.ts`) counts a failure as the second only within `INCIDENT_RESET_MS` (2 min) of the first; later it is a first failure again (retry in a minute) | C22 "a subtitle failure long after a successful retry is a first failure again …" |
| R1 hard card for queue waits | `start_stuck` for every state with a budget | hard card only for `planning`, `fallback`, `starting` (`queued`/`resolving` wait with "still preparing", within the server's own limits) | B16 "a start queued for minutes keeps waiting …" |
| R2 recurring step-down for server faults | "keeps coming back" for T5/T6/T7 | only device failures (T7, `playback_slideshow`) | C14 "three 503 incidents a few minutes apart: each one reloads, the method is never stepped down" |
| R3 conversion switch without budget | `switchPlayback` without the signal; no progress from its wait | `switchPlayback(…, signal)` (new optional parameter); the conversion's wait reports server states to `runner.progress()` | B25 "a new start whose audio conversion takes 70 s with server progress completes" |
| R4 restore without budget | the refused-switch catch path called `restore` without the switch budget's signal | passes it; a restore the spent budget aborted is the `step_timeout` card, never "aborted" | B19 "a refused viewer switch whose restore never answers ends on the step_timeout card" |
| R5 poster on every load | `lastFrame` ran on every hls.js load | only when the controller asks (`EngineSource.keepLastFrame`): a server switch under the switching card, never after a broken picture (`picture_black`/`frozen`, `video_stalled`); canvas capped at 640 px | E18 "a viewer switch keeps the last frame; a reload does not" (+ E18 poster test passes the flag) |
| R6 no web focus style | plain `Pressable` | focus ring (2 px `colors.focus`) on focus, transparent otherwise | player-status.test "keyboard focus on web shows the focus ring on the pill" |
| also | `stop()` left a viewer switch's polls running (found by the M17 row) | `stop()` cancels the switch budget (its signal aborts the switch's requests) | E18 "an engine 'ended' while a viewer switch runs …" |

**Test gaps (reviewer's survivors):** M04 → D34 "a frozen clock in the last seconds ends the title" (ended within 6 s; the mutant ends only via the hint path at 9 s); M05 → D35 "a
clock that jumps far (more than 3 s) is a seek"; M08 → D33 "web: the frame counter of a new source starts at 0";
M14 → B25 "playbackAlive false on the final stop report is no reason to restart"; M16 → E18 "an audio pick while a
